## G2 — CommanderProfiles, appointments, #6 guard

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/CommanderProfiles.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/CommanderAppointmentPlanner.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/AppointmentProtection.cs` (pure, persisted), `Strategic/Planner/CommanderAppointmentRuntime.cs` (runtime executor); rewrite `Patches/CommanderReplacementPatch.cs` (#6 → guard, P13); modify `Strategic/SuccessionScheduler.cs` (add `PriorFor`, see Steps; `MarkApplied` is called from the review's executor only), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (insert step 2 and the appointment executor step); test `CommanderPlannerTests.cs`. Plugin: bind `Enable Commander Appointment Planner`.

**Vanilla to read:** `GameVars.Commander` 58926-59026; getters 61134-61251 and `parentInfluence` 61043-61103 (the `commander < 0` → 0 case 61068-61070); `GetAverageAttributeValue` 59175; `CanBePickedForAIReplacement` 60748-60779; `CheckSuitableRank` 60556-60628; `IsServingUnderPlayer` 60695; `CheckAICommanderReplacements` 17008-17032; `UpdateCommanderRankingsCampaign` 84424-84487; `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit` 81389-81486; `AssignCommando` 60343 (+ Whiskey #30); `DoCommanderPromotion` 89077-89110; `ArmyGroup.AppointCommander` 75559-75580; `GetAvailableArmyGroupCommanders` 75546; `UpdateCommanderInChief` 81315-81387; `CheckVerticalReplacement` 17662-17687.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
// CommanderProfile itself is declared in G0's contracts (G3 consumes it before G2 merges).
public static class CommanderProfiles {
  public static CommanderProfile From(in CommanderRecord c);
  public static CommanderProfile Neutral { get; }
  public static CommanderProfile ForSector(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<CommanderRecord> cs, int commanderId); // Neutral when unknown
}
// As built (see "G2 as built"): sectors and the live vanilla predicate are inputs, the prior returns its event id.
public delegate float SuccessionPrior(int commanderId, string role, out int eventId); // SuccessionScheduler.PriorFor
public struct AppointmentIntent { public int CommanderId; public int TargetInstanceId; public bool TargetIsArmyGroup; public int DisplacedId;
  public int ReseatGroupInstanceId; /* the army group a succession figure leaves, reseated first; -1 none */ public int SuccessionEventId; /* -1 none */ public string Reason; /* "competence" | "succession:<id>" */ }
public static class CommanderAppointmentPlanner {
  public const float MinGain = 0.10f; /* = vanilla expdifferenceforverticalpromotion, so job 24 would agree */ public const float SuccessionPriorBonus = 0.25f;
  public const int MaxVanillaChecks = 8;
  public static string RoleOf(int alliance, WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.Theater theater, bool primary, bool inValley); // ANV/AoP, Valley, Western, null
  public static bool InShenandoahValley(float x, float z); // the retired army-area classifier's Valley box
  public static bool TryPlan(TheaterPlan plan, CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, SuccessionPrior successionPrior,
                             AppointmentProtection protection, System.Func<AppointmentIntent, bool> vanillaAllows, out AppointmentIntent intent); // at most one per review (P8)
}
public sealed class AppointmentProtectionBook { // both alliances' windows + each alliance's last review day (runtime-owned, CampaignScope)
  public AppointmentProtection For(int alliance); public int LastReviewDay(int alliance); public void Age(int alliance, int daySerial);
  public bool IsProtected(int alliance, int commanderId); } // as of the last review; false before the first
public static class AppointmentGuard { // the #6 guard's pure halves (P13)
  public static AppointmentGuardState ClearProtected(int[] worst, AppointmentProtectionBook book, int alliance); // the Prefix's whole decision
  public static AppointmentGuardState Clear(int[] worst, System.Func<int, bool> isProtected); public static int Restore(int[] worst, AppointmentGuardState state);
  public static bool VanillaWouldReplace(int bestId, int worstId, bool worstHasCommand, bool worstInGarrison, bool bestPickable, float bestAverage, float worstAverage, float margin); } // 17017-17020
// CommanderAssignmentGuard.ResolveReplacementAppointment(pickable, leadsOtherArmyGroup, freeApartFromArmyGroup, successorAvailable) (main's, kept): the executor's live reseat rule.
// Snapshot additions (G0 files, additive): CommanderRecord.Pickable, CommanderRecord.FreeApartFromArmyGroup; ArmyGroupRecord { InstanceId, CommanderId, X, Z, Strength, MemberInstanceIds }; CampaignSnapshot.ArmyGroups.
public sealed class AppointmentProtection { public const int ProtectionDays = 60;
  public void Protect(int commanderId, int daySerial); public bool IsProtected(int commanderId, int daySerial);
  public System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<int, int>> ProtectedUntil { get; } // commanderId → last protected day serial
  public void Prune(int daySerial); // ledger aging, every review
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<System.Collections.Generic.KeyValuePair<int, int>> protectedUntil); }
```
Appointment rule: candidate commands = the main-effort sector's top formations (army group, army, corps) in importance order; for each, the best available commander (`Available && !Navy && !Dummy && !RefreshCommand && !ServingUnderPlayer && !IsPlayer`, rank within the target unittyp's `RankBand` from the snapshot, the rank part of `CheckSuitableRank` 60556-60628; the remaining `CheckSuitableRank` terms are re-checked live by the executor before it acts) whose `Average + successionPrior − current.Average ≥ MinGain` displaces the current commander; never a player-owned command; never when the current commander is protected; for the W&L player's alliance in a career, never a candidate whose current command lies inside the target command's subtree (no subordinate-replaces-superior swap, P16, matching vanilla's 17664 exclusion), and never a target command whose subtree contains `CampaignSnapshot.PlayerCommandInstanceId`.

**Executor recipe (runtime, daily review):** army group → `ArmyGroup.AppointCommander(group, id, usefeudmechanism:false, resetcoordinationtime:true, ignoreprestige:true)` after reseating the appointee's old group from `GetAvailableArmyGroupCommanders` (as today's #6 does); corps/army HQ → `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit(appointee.currentcommand)` when he has one, `AssignCommando(target)`, `DoCommanderPromotion(id)` (clears `refreshcommand`, 89110). Then `AppointmentProtection.Protect`, `SuccessionScheduler.MarkApplied` for a succession reason, telemetry `[Appointment] alliance=<a> commander=<name> unit=<name> reason=<competence|succession:<id>> displaced=<name>`. Verify the result (`group.GetCommanderID()` / `target.commander`) before recording.

**#6 rewrite (guard):** Prefix on `CheckAICommanderReplacements(int _aifaction)` (17008): for the faction's alliance (0/1), set every slot of `GameVars.alliance[a].worstcommanderidperlevel` that holds a protected commander to −1 and record (slot, id) for exactly those slots in `__state`; the Finalizer writes each recorded id back into its slot and touches no other slot (vanilla skips a slot whose worst id is −1, 17017, so it never wrote those; slots vanilla consumed, both ids −1 at 17027-17028, stay as vanilla left them). No skip, no state write. `PatchFirstFire.Mark("campaign.patch.commander-replacement")` (id kept), scope `campaign.patch.commander-replacement`.

**Steps:**
- [x] Tests first: `ProfileHighInitiativeLowersRequiredOdds`, `ProfileNeutralForUnknownCommander`, `AppointmentNeedsMinGain`, `SuccessionPriorTipsANearTie`, `SuccessionPriorCannotBeatALargeCompetenceGap`, `NeverAppointsPlayerOrPlayerSubordinate`, `NeverTargetsPlayerOwnedCommand`, `ProtectedCommanderNotDisplaced`, `ProtectionExpiresAfterSixtyDays`, `GuardRestoresOnlyTheSlotsItCleared` (pure `AppointmentGuard.Clear/Restore` over an `int[]`: a slot vanilla set to −1 during the call stays −1), `OneAppointmentPerReview`, `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`, `NoVerticalSwapOnPlayerSideInCareer` (a subordinate inside the target's subtree is rejected for the player's alliance in a career, accepted for the enemy side and outside careers).
- [x] Implement pure files; `SuccessionScheduler` gains `float PriorFor(int alliance, string combinedName, string role, WarStateView w)` (fired-not-applied events give `SuccessionPriorBonus`) — the event list and gates stay; `CheckEvents` is unchanged.
- [x] Implement the executor and the #6 guard; delete #6's swap path (`TryApplyScriptedSwap`, `FindReplacementId`, `PickSuccessor` move into `CommanderAppointmentRuntime`; `AllianceGroups`, `BuildCandidates`, `IsPlayerGroup`, `GroupCommandedBy`, `FreeApartFromArmyGroup` move with them; `CommanderAssignmentGuard` keeps only what the executor and #30 call — orphan-sweep proves it). `Force All Succession Events` still marks all events fired (coordinator) and the appointment planner then treats them as fired-not-applied.
- [x] Gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): commander profiles, appointments, job-24 guard [G2]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness (`CommanderPlannerTests`); in-game at G7 (`[Appointment]` rows; a protected appointee is not replaced by vanilla job 24 within 60 days — `[first-fire] campaign.patch.commander-replacement` and no vanilla "AI: replacing commander <appointee>" debug line for him).

### G2 as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g2` (from `feat/campaign-planner` `dbc3a6f`). Harness 2334 PASS after verify round 1 (2299 + 38 in `CommanderPlannerTests` − 3 ported out of `CampaignMilitaryFixTests`; main's `SuccessionReseatsReplacementsOldArmyGroup` is kept), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (131 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `test_smoke_check.py` OK. One new first-fire id: `campaign.patch.commander-replacement.finalizer` (the Prefix keeps `campaign.patch.commander-replacement`); both fire on every job-24 pass of a normal campaign, so neither is situational.

Files: new `Strategic/Planner/{CommanderProfiles, CommanderAppointmentPlanner, AppointmentProtection}.cs` (pure, in the test csproj), `Strategic/Planner/CommanderAppointmentRuntime.cs` (runtime); rewritten `Patches/CommanderReplacementPatch.cs` (#6 guard); edited `Strategic/SuccessionScheduler.cs` (`PriorFor`; `GetPendingApplications` deleted), `Strategic/CommanderAssignmentGuard.cs` (swap-path helpers deleted; #30's `ResolvePreviousCommandAction`/`ShouldClearPreviousCommand` kept), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` + `CampaignSnapshot.cs` + `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (additive, below), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (`Age` in `ReviewPlan`, `Execute` in `ReviewExecute`), `Plugin.cs` (flag), `docs/patch-catalog.md` (#6 row, runtime row); tests `CommanderPlannerTests.cs` (new), `CampaignMilitaryFixTests.cs` (the role-resolution and reseat tests of the deleted helpers removed; their W&L and GiC assertions are ported into `NeverTargetsPlayerOwnedCommand`, `RoleFollowsTheaterAndPrimacy`, `SuccessionPriorOnlyForFiredNotApplied`).

Where the build differs from the text above (each checked against the decompile):
- **Army groups were not plannable from G0.** An army group is not a campaign group (`ArmyGroup` 75007; no `FormationRecord`), and its commander has no `currentcommand` (`AppointCommander` → `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit` 75575 frees his old one), so `TargetIsArmyGroup` could never be set. Added (additive): `ArmyGroupRecord { InstanceId, CommanderId, X, Z, Strength, MemberInstanceIds }` and `CampaignSnapshot.ArmyGroups`, read by `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.ReadArmyGroups` (members' mean position and summed `groupstrengthdirect`; `GetAlliance` 75528).
- **`CommanderRecord.Pickable`** (= `CanBePickedForAIReplacement(alliance, includeofficerswithcommand: true)`, 60748-60779) is added: the plan's filter (`Available && !Navy && !Dummy && !RefreshCommand && !ServingUnderPlayer && !IsPlayer`) did not exclude the CIC, staff officers or sitting army-group commanders, so the planner would have proposed the CIC every day and the executor refused him every day.
- **`TryPlan(plan, s, SectorModel sectors, SuccessionPrior prior, AppointmentProtection protection, Func<AppointmentIntent, bool> vanillaAllows, out AppointmentIntent intent)`**: `sectors` maps formations (`SectorOfArea(AreaIndex)`) and army groups (`SectorAt`) to the main-effort sector; `vanillaAllows` is the executor's live re-read of vanilla's rules (`CanBePickedForAIReplacement`, `!refreshcommand`, `CheckSuitableRank(unittyp, unit)` or `CheckSuitableRank(18)` as `GetAvailableArmyGroupCommanders` 75551 asks, target still under the snapshot's commander, nothing in battle), asked before an intent is returned so a refused pick tries the next candidate (≤ `MaxVanillaChecks` 8 per review) instead of blocking the day forever. The prior is a `SuccessionPrior(int commanderId, string role, out int eventId)` delegate (the executor needs the event id for `MarkApplied`); `AppointmentIntent` gains `SuccessionEventId`.
- **Targets and order:** the main-effort sector's army groups (18), armies (16) and corps (15), by echelon then strength; a formation in battle is skipped (vanilla's fallen-commander replacement also waits for the battle, 80022-80028). Rank: the band's maximum for every type, its minimum only for army groups (`CheckSuitableRank` 60577-60590; `DoCommanderPromotion` → `UpdateRankToFit` raises a junior appointee). A candidate already commanding a main-effort command of the same or a higher echelon is not moved (no churn inside the main effort); a corps commander may still take his army on the enemy side.
- **Score:** `GetAverageAttributeValue` + the prior for the target's role, for the candidate **and the sitting commander** (history is a weight: a historical figure in place keeps his bonus against a slightly better challenger). The live attribute getters are not part of the appointment score (they carry the parent-influence term of the current post, 61043-61103); the flag description says so.
- **Roles** (`CommanderAppointmentPlanner.RoleOf`, main's `ResolveRoleTargetGroup` rules, verify r1 F4 integrator decision): a main-effort command inside the Shenandoah Valley box (`InShenandoahValley`: x 950..1225, z −760..−400, the box of the retired `ArmyAreaClassifier` "ShenandoahValley" that G6 deletes, copied here) is `Valley`; the principal command is the first target outside the box (else the first target) → `ANV` (CSA) / `AoP` (Union) in the east; the principal western command → `Western`; a Valley event with no Valley command finds no role and defers; `GeneralInChief` never matches a command (C18). `SuccessionScheduler.PriorFor(int alliance, string combinedName, string role, out int eventId)` has no `WarStateView`: `CheckEvents` already applied the date and war-state gates when the event fired, so the parameter would have been dead.
- **Succession reseat (verify r1 F1, integrator decision: restored per spec component 7 and the executor recipe above).** Vanilla seats historical figures on auto-created army groups (`CreateNewArmyGroup` 75653) and its pool rejects every sitting army-group commander (`IsArmyGroupCommander`, 60770). So a commander outside the pool is still a candidate when a fired, unapplied succession event names him for the target's role, he is free apart from his army group (`CommanderRecord.FreeApartFromArmyGroup`: available, not serving under the player, not staff, not the CIC — main's `FreeApartFromArmyGroup`), his group is not a main-effort command and holds nothing of the player's, and the alliance is not the W&L player's (idle commanders only there, P16). The intent carries `ReseatGroupInstanceId`. The executor re-decides it live with main's `CommanderAssignmentGuard.ResolveReplacementAppointment` (kept): `ReseatThenAppoint` needs the group still his and free of the player, the figure free apart from it, and an idle successor (`PickSuccessor`: best `GetAverageAttributeValue` in `GetAvailableArmyGroupCommanders` 75546 who also passes `CanBePickedForAIReplacement` and is not `dlc_chosencommander`); with no idle successor it refuses (the planner tries the next candidate, so the event defers). It then reseats his old group (`AppointCommander(group, successor)`, verified; `AppointCommander` never clears the old group, 75559) and only then appoints him to the target. `[Appointment]` gains `reseated=<group>:<successor>` and every row carries `outcome=appointed`. When the reseat succeeds and the appointment then fails (verify r2 R2-F2), the row is still written with `outcome=appoint-failed-after-reseat` (nothing protected, the event not marked applied): the figure now has no command, so the next review sees him as an ordinary candidate in vanilla's pool (`Pickable`), and the event's prior still applies. The reseat's own result is verified (`group.GetCommanderID() == successor`) before he is appointed; that line is pinned. Deleted for good with the swap path: `RoleIsArmyGroupCommand`, `ResolveRoleTargetGroup`, `ArmyGroupRoleCandidate`, `GroupCommandedBy` and `BuildCandidates`' `ArmyAreaRuntime.AreaKey` read.
- **`refreshcommand`:** the plan says `DoCommanderPromotion` clears it (89110); vanilla's job 24 sets it to `true` right after (17026), as `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit` does (81455, 81478), and `CheckCommanderPromotions` (89066) clears it later. The executor mirrors job 24 exactly: `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit(old)` → `AssignCommando(target)` → `DoCommanderPromotion` → `refreshcommand = true`. After `ReplaceCommanderOfUnit` the appointee has no `currentcommand`, so #30's Postfix finds no stale previous command.
- **W&L (P16, C19).** On the W&L player's alliance in a career (a commander of the alliance is the player) only idle commanders (no `currentcommand`) are candidates: that is vanilla's job-24 pool on that side (84467-84473) and adds no subordinate-replaces-superior swap, which vanilla withholds there (17664, BUG-WL-014, user: leave as vanilla). A subtree test could not do it: `Own` holds campaign groups only, so a non-top division's commander has no command in the snapshot. A target is never a formation of stage F's player chain classes (`PlayerCommand`, `PlayerMapGroup`, `CarryingGroup`, `CicSide`), `PlayerCommandInstanceId`, or any ancestor of those by `ParentInstanceId`, nor an army group with such a member; the sitting commander is never the player or one serving under him; the player or his officers are never candidates; the executor also refuses `dlc_chosencommander` and a `dlcw_isundercommander` target live.
- **No profiles step in `ReviewPlan`.** Nothing in G2 reads a `CommanderProfile`; G3's runtime passes `CommanderProfiles.ForSector` to `TheaterPlanner.Plan`. `ReviewPlan` gains `CommanderAppointmentRuntime.Age` before the bookkeeping return (protection aging runs during the W&L pause too, P1), which also records the review's day serial: the #6 guard runs inside vanilla's job 24 between reviews and tests protection against that day.
- **Guard wiring (verify r1 F2).** The protection rule is pure: `AppointmentProtectionBook` (both alliances' windows and each alliance's last review day; `Age` records the day and prunes; `IsProtected(alliance, id)` is as of that day and false before the first review) and `AppointmentGuard.ClearProtected(worst, book, alliance)`. The runtime only owns the book (`CommanderAppointmentRuntime.Book`, reset by CampaignScope) and delegates `Age`; the Prefix's whole decision is that one `ClearProtected` call (pinned verbatim). When a cleared slot is one vanilla would really have swapped (`AppointmentGuard.VanillaWouldReplace`, its 17017-17020 test, with `GamePrefs.expdifferenceforverticalpromotion`), the Prefix logs `[once:replace:blocked]`: the smoke proof of a real job-24 block.
- **Executor gate:** `CommanderAppointmentRuntime.Enabled()` = `Enable Campaign Planner` && `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` && `Enable Commander Appointment Planner`, read by the executor and the #6 guard (the guard writes vanilla's array for the call, so it is an executor write). `Execute` also requires today's plan (`plan.DaySerial == s.DaySerial`).
- **Protection is in memory until G6** (sidecar v3 `appointments`): a save and reload inside an appointee's 60 days leaves him unprotected from job 24 until then. The appointment step has its own try/catch (`campaign-planner:appointment-step-failed`) so a failure there never skips the G5 executors that follow it in `ReviewExecute`.
- **In game before G3 merges** the published plan has `MainSectorId = -1`, so nothing is appointed and the succession swaps the old #6 made stop until G3 publishes a main effort (the branch is not merged to `main` before then). #16 (`ArmyGroupManagementPatch.AppointPreferredCommander`) can still re-seat its preferred historical name on an army group it attaches units to; the #6 guard covers job 24 only. G6 migrates #16.

TEST-ONLY members added by G2 (orphan-sweep `--include-test-only` 72 → 65 (unchanged list; every r1 addition has a src reader) after verify r1; ten G0 rows now have src readers: `CampaignPlanStore.TryGetPlan/TryGetSectors`, `SectorModel.SectorAt/SectorOfArea/TheaterOf`, `TheaterPlan.MainSectorId`, `CommanderProfile` and its three fields): `CommanderProfiles` / `ForSector` (and `From`, `Neutral`, which the name-based sweep does not list) → G3 `TheaterPlanner.Plan` via the runtime; `AppointmentProtection.ProtectedUntil` (and `Restore`) → G6 sidecar v3 `appointments`.

Mutation plants (scratch copy, one at a time, harness run with the scratch copy as working directory; `evidence/g2/mutation-results.txt`):
- planner: `MinGain` 0 → `AppointmentNeedsMinGain` (and 7 more); the sitting commander without his prior → `SittingSuccessionFigureKeepsHisBonus`; player's side may move sitting commanders → `NoVerticalSwapOnPlayerSideInCareer`; no ancestor walk in the player-containing set → `NeverTargetsPlayerOwnedCommand`; the band minimum for every type → `RankBandMinimumOnlyForArmyGroups` (survived the first pass because the corps took the junior commander; the test now pins the army target; killed on rerun); main-effort commander pulled sideways → `MainEffortCommanderNotPulledSideways` (rerun after the rule moved onto `CommandUnitTyp`); a target in battle kept → `TargetInBattleSkipped`; alliance bound dropped → `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`; the player may be displaced → `NeverDisplacesThePlayer`; a protected sitter displaced → `ProtectedCommanderNotDisplaced`; `Pickable` ignored → `OnlyVanillasAppointmentPoolIsCandidate` (survived the first pass; the test was added and kills it).
- protection and guard: the guard clears every slot → `GuardRestoresOnlyTheSlotsItCleared`; window one day longer → `ProtectionExpiresAfterSixtyDays`; `PriorFor` ignores applied events → `SuccessionPriorOnlyForFiredNotApplied`.
- verify round 1 (`evidence/g2/mutation-results-r2.txt`): the verifier's surviving plants, re-run with the scratch copy as working directory, all killed: M8 Finalizer returns `null` → `GuardPatchNeverSkipsVanilla`; M9 cap removed → `VanillaChecksCappedAtEight`; M10 the book's `Age` does not record the day → `ProtectionBookCountsFromTheLastReview` (and the runtime `Age` not reaching the book → `AppointmentStepWiredIntoTheReview`); M11 prune drops the last protected day → `ProtectionExpiresAfterSixtyDays`; M13 the live `dlc_chosencommander` refusal dropped → `ExecutorMirrorsVanillaRecipe`; M15 the patch guard ignores protection → `GuardPatchNeverSkipsVanilla`, the pure predicate inert or reading the wrong alliance → `GuardClearsThisAlliancesProtectedOnly`. New plants: the block test without its margin → `VanillaWouldReplaceMirrorsJob24`; reseat without a succession event → `SuccessionReseatMovesAFigureOffHisArmyGroup` (survived once: the test's figure lacked the competence to win anyway; a far better un-named figure now pins it); reseat on the player's side, of the player's group, or pulling a main-effort group commander → `SuccessionReseatNeverOnThePlayersSideOrGroup` (the last survived once: the test's target had no role; a second main-effort army group now carries ANV); reseat ignoring free-apart → `SuccessionReseatDefersWithoutIdleSuccessor`; the intent losing its group → `SuccessionReseatMovesAFigureOffHisArmyGroup`; Valley without the box or for any secondary eastern command → `ValleyRoleNeedsAShenandoahCommand`; runtime: reseat not decided live, the player's group reseated, a successor outside vanilla's AI pool, appointing without reseating → `ExecutorMirrorsVanillaRecipe`. M6 (the restore's −1 check) and M12 (the planner's CIC check, redundant with `ReviewMode`) stay unobservable/redundant, as the verifier recorded.
- verify round 2 (`evidence/g2/mutation-results-r3.txt`): N3 the reseat result not verified before appointing → `ExecutorMirrorsVanillaRecipe` (killed; survived round 2 before the pin); the partial reseat row dropped → same.
- runtime wiring (source pins): `Execute` not called from `ReviewExecute` → `AppointmentStepWiredIntoTheReview`; `Age` after the bookkeeping return → same; the appointment flag not read by `Enabled()` → same; the guard Prefix returns `false` → `GuardPatchNeverSkipsVanilla`; the guard marks a succession applied → same; no `refreshcommand = true` → `ExecutorMirrorsVanillaRecipe`; `Protect` before the verified result → same; `CanBePickedForAIReplacement` not re-checked live → same.

G2 smoke items (checked with G7's smoke; `LOG` and `SESSION` as there):
1. `LOG`: `[first-fire] campaign.patch.commander-replacement` and `[first-fire] campaign.patch.commander-replacement.finalizer`; absent: `replace:prefix`, `replace:finalizer`, `campaign-planner:appointment-step-failed`, `campaign-planner:appointment-failed`, `campaign-planner:appointment-check-failed`, `campaign-planner:appointment-unit-failed`, `campaign-planner:appointment-group-failed`, `campaign-planner:appointment-reseat-failed`.
2. `SESSION/campaign*.jsonl` (after G3 publishes a main effort): `[Appointment] alliance=<a> commander=<name> unit=<main-effort army group/army/corps> reason=<competence|succession:<id>> displaced=<name> outcome=appointed` at most once per alliance per day; none for the W&L CIC player's alliance and none on either side while the W&L pause line holds.
3. Protection: once an appointee is protected, `[once:replace:guard] [Patch:Replace] job 24 skips the campaign planner's protected appointees (P13)`, and a real job-24 block must appear in the session: `[once:replace:blocked] [Patch:Replace] job 24 would have replaced a protected appointee; the guard kept him (P13)` (vanilla's own swap test held for a cleared slot); in vanilla's debug log no `AI: replacing commander <appointee>` line for 60 days after his `[Appointment]` row. A session with appointments but no `replace:blocked` line in 3 months proves nothing about the guard: record it as unproven, not passed.
6. Succession reseat: an `[Appointment] … reason=succession:<id> … reseated=<group>:<successor> outcome=appointed` row (an `outcome=appoint-failed-after-reseat` row is a partial move to look into) when a fired event names a figure who sits on an army group; never on the W&L player's alliance; `campaign-planner:appointment-reseat-failed` absent.
4. W&L brigade/division career: no `[Appointment]` names the player, an officer serving under him, his carrying group or any formation above it; on the player's alliance every `[Appointment]` appointee was idle (no `currentcommand`) before it.
5. Rollback: `Enable Commander Appointment Planner = false` → no `[Appointment]` rows, no `replace:guard` line; vanilla job 24 unchanged.

Integration with G1 (merged after G1 into `feat/campaign-planner`, 2026-10-15): `ReviewPlan` runs snapshot → G1 detection and beliefs → stand-down return → `CommanderAppointmentRuntime.Age` → bookkeeping return. G2's early stand-down return before the snapshot is gone (G1's integrator decision: detection runs for the W&L CIC player's alliance); the stand-down alliance's protection windows are still not aged, as on G2 (`ReviewExecute` never appoints for it). Every G2 day value is the snapshot's `DaySerial`, which since G1 (verify-g1 R2-3) counts calendar days: a protection window is 60 calendar days, and `AppointmentProtection`'s comment no longer describes the 372 scale.

## G3 — HistoricalPriorTable, TheaterPlanner, depot tasks

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/HistoricalPriorTable.cs`, `Strategic/Planner/TheaterPlanner.cs`, `Strategic/Planner/PlannerState.cs` (hysteresis + RNG serial + campaign seed, persisted), `Strategic/Planner/RiverLines.cs` (`ITerrainLineProbe`, `EffectiveDistance`; G0 landing B) (all pure); `Strategic/Planner/TerrainLineRuntime.cs` (runtime `ITerrainLineProbe` over `BattleUnits.bfs.CountTerrainLine(start, angle, distance, GameVars.terrainrestrictions_combat) < distance`, the 80517 test, results memoized per review day and capped per review); `Strategic/Planner/LogisticsGate.DepotTasks.cs` (the `AddDepotTasks` part of G0's partial class), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (steps 3-4, written after G2 merges: it passes `CommanderProfiles.ForSector`); test `TheaterPlannerTests.cs`. Reads (no edit): `HistoricalOperationCatalog.BuildProfiles` (source data for the prior table; G6 deletes the catalog after copying the table), `ContactEvidenceLedger` (adopted here as the sector contact class), `BattleHistoryQuery`, `TheaterClassifier.FromPosition`.

**Vanilla to read:** `CalculateMostValueableAIZones` 10964-11009 (value terms); `GetClosestAreaDistance` 14835; `UpdateIIPSupplyLink` 114074-114500 (supply reach; 114272-114306); `GetGroupSupplyState` 14063-14066; `SupplyInZoneAvailable` 10941-10962; `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` 11712-11741 (capital need formula); `IDAccomplished` / `GetAvailableObjectives`; `CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` 25608-25671; `IsTerrainPositionRestricted` 25755-25806.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
// SectorModel, ReachResult, LogisticsGate.Evaluate and LogisticsGate.ClampToReach are G0 contracts.
public static partial class LogisticsGate { // LogisticsGate.DepotTasks.cs (as built: the caller passes the axis; see G3 as built)
  public static void AddDepotTasks(CampaignSnapshot s, TheaterPlan plan, SectorModel sectors, float frontierX, float frontierZ, float toX, float toZ, float formationCommandRange, float supplySlack); // ≤ 2 tasks per plan
}
public static class HistoricalPriorTable { public struct Entry { public string Id; public int Alliance; public int[] ObjectiveIds; public int StartSerial, EndSerial; public int Priority; }
  public static System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<Entry> Entries { get; }
  public static float Weight(in Entry e, int daySerial); // 1 inside, 0.5^(monthsOutside/3) outside, × Priority/3
  public static float PriorFor(int alliance, int objectiveId, int daySerial, out string id); }
public sealed class PlannerState { public int CurrentMainSector = -1; public int CurrentMainObjective = -1; public int ChallengerObjective = -1; public int ChallengerDays;
  public int DecisionSerial; public long CampaignSeed; public int BelowOddsDays; public bool CapitalThreatLatched; /* verify r1 F1/F2 */
  public void Restore(int mainSector, int mainObjective, int challengerObjective, int challengerDays, int decisionSerial, long campaignSeed,
                      int belowOddsDays = 0, bool capitalThreatLatched = false); }
public interface ITerrainLineProbe { bool Blocks(float ax, float az, float bx, float bz); bool RiverNear(float x, float z, float range); } // the planner's view (80502-80517; 26727)
public interface ITerrainLineSource { bool TryLine(...); bool TryRiver(...); bool ProbeLine(...); bool ProbeRiver(...); } // memo lookups free, probes budgeted (TerrainLineRuntime)
public static class RiverLines { // a river delays a threat, it never removes one
  public static float EffectiveDistance(float ax, float az, float bx, float bz, ITerrainLineProbe probe, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<CrossingRecord> crossings); }
internal static class TheaterPlanner { // internal: BattleHistoryRecord is internal (CS0051)
  public const float OddsDeadband = 0.10f, CapitalThreatRelease = 0.4f, MarchHorizonDays = 30f; /* verify r1 F1, F2, F4 */
  public const float ValueWeight = 0.30f, BalanceWeight = 0.25f, ReachWeight = 0.15f, DistanceWeight = 0.10f, ExposureWeight = 0.10f, CapitalThreatWeight = 0.10f;
  public const float PriorLambda = 0.08f, PriorMaxContribution = 0.08f, SwitchMargin = 0.12f, Epsilon = 0.04f, Temperature = 0.02f, CapitalThreatTrigger = 0.6f;
  public const int SwitchDays = 3; public const float HoldRatio = 0.6f; public const float RecoverSupplyBelow = 0.4f; /* raw Supply01 */
  internal static TheaterPlan Plan(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, System.Func<int, CommanderProfile> sectorProfile /* CommanderProfiles.ForSector (G2) */,
                                 PlannerState state, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<BattleHistoryRecord> battles,
                                 ITerrainLineSource terrain, in MarchSpeedInputs speed, out string trace);
  internal static float MassFor(Inputs c, float tx, float tz, int targetSector, System.Collections.Generic.List<int> into); // the massable set (F4)
}
```
Score per available objective (one per sector candidate): value (objective importance + role bonus), believed balance at it (**massable own strength** M ÷ (M + the river-weighted believed land enemy within relocation range of the target); amended at verify r1 F4: M is the strength G4 can concentrate there — own formations that are movable (no busy flag), committed (`WlChainRules.CountsAsCommittedDefender`: never the W&L player's command or map group), not Recover (raw `Supply01` ≥ 0.4, morale ≥ `aiminimummoraleformovement`) and within `MarchHorizonDays` (30) of the target by the march-time estimate, less the capital reserve (the nearest such formations to the capital up to `CapitalFloorStrength`, G4's CapitalGuard rule) and less every other sector's hold reserve (what holds there anyway, then its massable formations nearest the sector's believed army up to its `HoldNeed`, G4's Hold withholding rule; released for a target its threat sits at); the main target's set is published as `TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds`/`MassStrength` and eligibility compares M ÷ believed enemy with `RequiredRatio`), reach (`LogisticsGate.Evaluate` for the strongest candidate formation; out of reach → score 0 for advance, depot task instead), march time (1 − normalized **march-time estimate** of the candidate force, G0 landing B: XZ distance at the P6 speed chain without the bound's maxima — `unitbasicspeed[t,1] × groupspeed × basicunitspeed × generalcampaignunitspeedfactor / terrainspeccost[7]` on roads — with river and rail segments credited at the cost-1 rate when the force is within its `commanderrange` of a river cell or a completed own rail line running toward the target (sampled along the line with `BattlefieldSetup.GetCurrentTerrainOnPos`, 26727), rail only when `UseRails` and the alliance's train capacity covers the force: `transportcapacity[0] − transportcapacityused[0] ≥ usedtransportcapacitypermenstrength[0] × strength / railroadspeedadj / terrainspeccost[0] × terrainspeccost[9]`, vanilla's own entrain test 116420-116446; so a rail or river corridor makes a far sector fast and rail becomes a strategic mover), exposure (believed enemy strength near the axis flank), capital threat (own capital: believed enemy strength within relocation range ÷ own floor; enemy capital: bonus), + prior. **River lines (G0 landing B):** vanilla starts a campaign battle only when `CountTerrainLine` from the caller toward the enemy over `terrainrestrictions_combat` {1,3,4,5,6,8} (64535) reaches the full bugle range (`EstablishCampaignBattle` 80683-80687 → `CheckIfUnitIsWithinModifiedRange` 80502-80517; `CountTerrainLine` 27695), so a river line is a real engagement barrier that an army crosses at a ford or bridge. Every believed-enemy distance in balance, exposure, capital threat and Hold need, and the own advance's march time, goes through `RiverLines.EffectiveDistance`: the straight distance when `ITerrainLineProbe.Blocks` is false, else the shortest detour through a `CampaignSnapshot.Crossings` cell (fords and bridges, 26628-26638); with no crossing the straight distance stays (a river delays a threat, it never removes one). **Afloat enemies (G0 landing B):** a believed enemy with `EnemyTraits.Embarked` cannot be engaged by land (`EstablishCampaignBattle` returns false when either side has `currentmodeltype` 1 or 2, 80632-80635) and moves at the cost-1 river/sea rate: it counts in capital threat and exposure as a moving threat, never as a holdable contact in Hold need or the balance at a land objective. `Tempo` is null on the first review (`DirectorMemories[a].LastPosture` before `PublishDirectorPosture` has run): G3 and G4 read null as both modifiers 0. Required ratio: `RequiredRatio = OffensiveDominance × (1 + Tempo.MassRatioModifier) + RequiredOddsShift` (snapshot `OffensiveDominance` = `aidominanceforoffensiveoperations`; tempo from the director, C17; the sector commander's profile), and `AttackBias` scales the balance term; `SupplySlack` widens `LogisticsGate.Evaluate`'s reach. Postures: MainEffort sector; Support = sectors adjacent to the main axis; Hold = sectors with believed enemy and own formations; EconomyOfForce = sectors with own formations and no believed threat; Screen = sectors whose own strength is below their hold need `(HoldRatio + Tempo.MinimumHoldRatioModifier) × believed land threat` (corrected at G3 as built; the literal "enemy stronger than HoldRatio × own" made even odds a Screen), and threatened sectors with no own formation; every `SectorPlan` carries `HoldNeed`; Recover = sectors whose formations average raw `Supply01` < `RecoverSupplyBelow` (0.4 raw, i.e. 0.2 on vanilla's halved scale) or morale below `aiminimummoraleformovement`.

### G3 as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g3` from `feat/campaign-planner` `dbc3a6f`, built in parallel with G1, G2 and G1b-pure. Harness 2341 PASS (2299 + 42 in `TheaterPlannerTests`), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (130 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 72 → 55 (G3 gives src readers to 17 G0 test-only members and adds none; name-based, see the census rows below for the members a name collision hides), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new patch, no new first-fire id, no new flag.

Files: new pure `Strategic/Planner/{HistoricalPriorTable, PlannerState, RiverLines, MarchTime, TheaterPlanner, LogisticsGate.DepotTasks}.cs` (test csproj), runtime `Strategic/Planner/TerrainLineRuntime.cs`; edited `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (plan step, battle inbox, `[CampaignPlan]` row), `CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` + `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (`FormationRecord.GroupSpeed`, one field and one read), `StrategicCoordinator.cs` (one line: `CampaignPlannerRuntime.OnBattleRecorded(record)` after `BattleHistory.Add`); test `TheaterPlannerTests.cs`.

**What waits for G2:** one line. `CampaignPlannerRuntime.SectorProfileUntilG2` (marked `// G2 SEAM`, pinned to occur once by `PlannerPlanStepPinned`) hands `TheaterPlanner.Plan` an all-zero `CommanderProfile` (no odds shift, attack bias or supply slack). On merge it is deleted, the call passes `id => CommanderProfiles.ForSector(snapshot.Commanders, id)`, and `TheaterPlannerTests.PlannerPlanStepPinned` is updated with it (its expected call text names `SectorProfileUntilG2`, and its seam count goes from 1 to 0). G2's `CommanderProfiles.Neutral` is `new CommanderProfile { CommanderId = -1 }` (all zero, as the seam's); what differs after the merge is that `ForSector` returns `CommanderProfiles.From` of the real commander, and `From` gives an average commander (initiative and leadership 0.5) `AttackBias` 0.25 by the contract formula, so pre-merge plans are the no-personality case; nothing reads the plan before G5 (corrected at verify r1, F8). Everything else of G3, including the runtime plan step and the battle forward, is built and wired now: the plan's DAG put the runtime step after G2 only because of this one delegate, and `TerrainLineRuntime` would otherwise have no src reader. **Done at the G3 merge into `feat/campaign-planner` (after G1, G2, G1b):** the seam is deleted, the call passes `id => CommanderProfiles.ForSector(snapshot.Commanders, id)`, and `PlannerPlanStepPinned` checks that call text and a seam count of 0. In `ReviewPlan`, `TerrainLineRuntime.PrepareDrain(snapshot)` runs after G1's detection and stand-down return and G2's `CommanderAppointmentRuntime.Age`, before the bookkeeping return.

**`main` 8099aaa merged into `feat/campaign-planner` (perf hot paths: frame-budgeted daily review with atomic publish; transpiler label hotfix; arms #105-#108).** The single-frame review became the job list of `DailyReviewScheduler`; the planner's steps run as two jobs per alliance in the single-frame order: `Plan` (after Policy, before Front: `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewPlan`, also for a W&L CIC player's alliance as bookkeeping) and `Execute` (after Defense, before Formation: `ReviewExecute`); the list is 25 jobs. G1's calendar-day stamp (`EnemyDetectionRuntime.StampReviewDay`) runs in the first job (Map), before any reader, whatever the readiness; the calendar day, the W&L job-machine pause (spec C5) and the career-not-started deferral are captured with the day's inputs in `BeginReviewDay`, and each order-issuing job tests the captured pause (`CoordinatorPauseGuardsOrderIssuingSteps`). The sliced==unsliced model (`SlicedMatchesUnsliced`) carries the planner's out-of-set state (plan from today's posture and policy and yesterday's front; executors from today's plan and defense intent) and compares it per day. Judgement calls: (a) main's published succession set (`SuccessionScheduler.PublishFired`, `GetPendingApplications`) served the old #6 swap path, which G2 deleted; it is removed with its test (`SuccessionAppliesOnlyPublishedEvents`), and G2's `PriorFor` keeps reading the working `FiredEventIds` inside the review job, as the single-frame review did. (b) `CampaignPlanStore` and the belief model are written inside the Plan job and are not part of the atomic `StrategicLedgerSet` publish; today nothing outside a review job reads the plan store, while beliefs are read outside the review by `RecruitmentPatch` and `FortConstructionGovernorPatch` (`EnemyDetectionRuntime.BeliefsFor`), which between the Plan job and the commit see today's beliefs next to yesterday's published ledgers for a few frames (`RecruitmentPatch` also reads the published CIC and formation directives); recorded, not changed: making the belief model part of the commit is a planner-wide change for the integrator. **G5 obligation:** a patch that reads `CampaignPlanStore` (the #2 pointer Postfix, G5a/G5b/G5c) must read a copy published at the day's commit, or the plan store joins `StrategicLedgerSet`'s publish.

Where the build differs from the text above (each checked against the decompile or the shipped code):
- **`TheaterPlanner` is `internal`** (`BattleHistoryRecord` is internal: CS0051), and `Plan` takes two more inputs: `Plan(s, sectors, sectorProfile, state, battles, ITerrainLineSource terrain, in MarchSpeedInputs speed, out trace)`. The runtime passes `TerrainLineRuntime.ForReview()` and `TerrainLineRuntime.ReadMarchSpeed(alliance)`.
- **`ITerrainLineProbe` has a second method**, `RiverNear(x, z, range)`: the march-time river corridor needs "a river cell within commanderrange" (terrain 3/4/5, the cells River Expedition speeds, 133482-133491), one runtime probe for both questions.
- **`MarchTime.cs` + `MarchSpeedInputs`** (new pure file, not in the owned list): `unitbasicspeed[t,1] (133350-133354) × groupspeed (133991) × basicunitspeed (133535; unitprefs 853-854 = 1000) × generalcampaignunitspeedfactor (134083; 30) / terrainspeccost / 365.25` per day (campaign time is floating years, 30171). Road cost `terrainspeccost[7]`; river leg at `terrainspeccost[4]` (the leading run of samples, every commanderrange from the formation, at most 16, with `RiverNear`) when `UseRivers`; rail leg at `× railroadspeedadj / terrainspeccost[9]` (133401-133404) when `UseRails`, a completed line whose two endpoint IIPs are own-owned has an endpoint within commanderrange and its other endpoint nearer the target (leg = the distance gained, capped at the line length), and vanilla's entrain test passes (`transportcapacity[0] − transportcapacityused[0] ≥ usedtransportcapacitypermenstrength[0] × strength / railroadspeedadj / terrainspeccost[0] × terrainspeccost[9]`, 116434-116441, without the unit's own `usedtransportcapacity`, 0 off the train). The two cost-1 modes exclude each other: the larger saving is taken.
- **`FormationRecord.GroupSpeed`** (G0 contract, additive): `Regiment.groupspeed`, the slowest member's `unitbasicspeed[t,1]` (120438-120440), starting at `unitbasicspeed[15,1]` (120179). A constant would make every formation march equally.
- **`LogisticsGate.AddDepotTasks(s, plan, sectors, frontierX, frontierZ, toX, toZ, commandRange, supplySlack)`**: the G0 signature has no axis. `Plan` calls it when the main target is out of reach, after clamping the axis once: the frontier is the farthest in-reach point on the axis (`ClampToReach`), else the force's position (job 19 wishes at the unit's position, 14770-14775), and it is also the main sector's `TargetX/Z`, so the movers never outrun supply while `MainObjectiveId` stays. Site 1 is the frontier, site 2 one reach range on when the target lies beyond that; a site within reach range of an own or allied depot (built or not) is skipped. Priority 1, then 0.5.
- **Prior tier** (`HistoricalPriorTable`): the catalog's `Priority` is 10/15/20 sorted ascending (`CompareMatches`, `HistoricalOperationCatalog.cs:247`: 10 is the most important), while the plan's `× Priority / 3` assumes 1..3. The table stores tier 3/2/1 for 10/15/20, so `Weight ≤ 1` and `PriorLambda × Weight ≤ PriorMaxContribution`; `Plan` also caps the term at `PriorMaxContribution`. Window = day 1 of the start month to the end month's last day (`DaySerial(1, endMonth + 1, endYear) − 1`, as the catalog compares months); outside it the decay counts months of `MeanMonthDays`, the serial's own mean month over a four-year leap cycle (30.44 days on G1's calendar `DaySerial`, 31 on the legacy one), so the table is right under either serial (amended at verify r1 for G1 verify r3 R3-2; `PriorDecaysOutsideWindow` checks a 30-day month's end, and every G3 test passes against G1 `9cabeaa`'s calendar `ReviewCadenceCatchUp.DaySerial`). The six rows are copied literally; `PriorTableMatchesCatalog` pins every row (alliance, allow list, window, tier) against `HistoricalOperationCatalog.TryGetById` until G6 deletes the catalog.
- **Screen posture** reads "own below the hold need": `own < (HoldRatio + Tempo.MinimumHoldRatioModifier) × believed land threat`. The literal "a believed enemy stronger than HoldRatio × own" would make even odds a Screen, against G4's hold need `HoldRatio × believed enemy`; `ScreenOnlyBelowHoldNeedParityIsHold` pins parity as Hold. A sector with a believed land threat and no own formation is Screen (G1b gives it a screen task); a sector with neither gets no `SectorPlan`.
- **Capital threat and floor are one computation** (spec "÷ own floor" was ambiguous): `CapitalFloorStrength = CapitalFloorRatio (1.0) × Σ confidence × strength / max(10, d)^0.3 × 0.5` over beliefs within relocation range of the own capital (vanilla's 11712-11741 weighting, `d` the river-effective distance for land beliefs, straight for afloat ones); `threat01 = clamp01(floor / own Σ min(str, str × morale) within relocation range of the capital, at current positions)`. Vanilla instead subtracts `str × morale` of its own units whose last waypoint is nearer the capital than the nearest enemy (11744-11749); Whiskey reads current positions, never a unit's destination (record corrected at verify r1, F8). Trigger at `CapitalThreatTrigger` 0.6. The candidate term is `threat01 × clamp01(1 − d(target, own capital) / 2R)`, or 1 for an enemy capital.
- **Exposure enters as `ExposureWeight × (1 − exposure)`** so the live score stays in [0, 1] (the same ranking as subtracting it).
- **Eligibility (revised at verify r1, F4):** a candidate may become the main effort only when its massable own strength M (`MassFor`, defined in the balance term above) over the river-weighted believed land enemy near it reaches `RequiredRatio`: that is how a profile's `RequiredOddsShift` and the tempo change behaviour (`BoldCommanderAcceptsWorseOdds`), and separated corps that can concentrate count together (`EligibilityCountsMassableStrength`). The candidate force is the nearest member of the mass with at least `MainBodyShare` 0.5 of its largest member's strength. With no believed enemy at the target the odds are unbounded when a force exists.
- **Triggers (revised at verify r1, F1/F2):** a current main effort below `RequiredRatio − OddsDeadband` (0.10), or below `RequiredRatio` on `SwitchDays` consecutive reviews (`PlannerState.BelowOddsDays`), is dropped (`switch=odds`); inside the deadband it is kept. The capital trigger fires on onset only and is latched in `PlannerState.CapitalThreatLatched` until the threat falls below `CapitalThreatRelease` (0.4); while it stands the margin rule applies as normal. A trigger keeps the current main effort while it is within `Epsilon` of the best (`TriggerKeepsCurrentWithinEpsilon`). Randomness only at a decision; the daily challenger test uses the deterministic best. Candidates are kept in a fixed order (objective id, position), so neither tie-breaks nor the seeded draw depend on vanilla's objective order.
- **Value term:** `0.5 × Importance01 + 0.25 × the target area's importancevalues / the largest + 0.25 × role` (capital 1; rail hub, port, industry 0.5 each, capped at 1).
- **River-weighted threat:** a land belief counts when its straight distance is inside the radius, weighted `min(1, radius / RiverLines.EffectiveDistance)`; a blocked line detours through the nearest crossing (one crossing, legs not re-probed: crossing cells 5/6 are themselves in `terrainrestrictions_combat`, 64535); no crossing keeps the straight distance. Afloat beliefs (`EnemyTraits.Embarked`, 80632-80635) count at straight distance in the capital threat and the axis exposure only.
- **River lines are order-independent and all-or-nothing (revised at verify r1, F3; integrator decision).** `Plan` asks every terrain question through `ReviewTerrain` (pure, `RiverLines.cs`) over an `ITerrainLineSource` (`TerrainLineRuntime`): a memo lookup is free; an unknown answer is recorded and the review is then scored with **no** river term for any candidate (straight distances, no river corridor), and the unknown questions are probed in a fixed order (capital, candidates by ascending objective id and position, sectors by id; inside each in the snapshot's belief order) within the budget. Questions are asked between **AI area centres** (each point's closest area by vanilla's `GetClosestAreaDistance`, 14835; a line inside one area is clear; a river point is its area's centre): exact endpoints re-key every day an army moves, so a per-review budget could never complete the memo, while area centres are static (11259-11286) and the question set of a campaign is finite (area pairs within `MaxProbedLine` 600, areas × command ranges). Lines longer than 600 are never probed and read as clear in every review alike (a line longer than a review's step budget could never complete; a threat line joins points within the relocation range 275, so between area centres it is shorter in practice unless an area is very large; long marches lose the detour). The probe itself is exactly 80511-80517 (`CountTerrainLine(start, Tools.GetAngle(start, end) + 180f, distance, GameVars.terrainrestrictions_combat) < distance`), memoized for the campaign (CampaignScope `campaign-planner-terrain-probe`; the terrain-spec grid is static) on a 16-unit grid, at most 50000 entries. Per review it spends at most 1500 map units of `CountTerrainLine`, 128 river points and 1.0 ms of probing time (a CPU budget; after the review's first probe a line is not started when its length × the measured cost per step would overrun it; the first probe always runs, so the head of the fixed order can never be refused forever and a review's probing costs at most the budget or one line of ≤ 600 steps); a refused probe changes no answer the plan uses. An unknown question is recorded once per review (`unknown=` counts distinct questions). **The memo is filled outside the reviews (integrator decision after verify r1):** `TerrainMemoDrain` (pure, `RiverLines.cs`) holds the campaign's whole static question set, every pair of AI area centres within 600 (the only lines a review can ask, so moving beliefs never add one) and every area centre at every own command range seen (river points; a new range appends its points), in a fixed order (lines by area list position, then river points by range). Each review's snapshot prepares it (`TerrainLineRuntime.PrepareDrain`; the map's pairs once, so it starts with the first review after a load), and it is drained a frame at a time (`TerrainLineRuntime.DrainTick`, scope `campaign.planner.terrain-drain`; per frame 1500 steps, 128 river points and 0.9 ms of probing, `MaxDrainMillisPerFrame`, a margin under the 1 ms target), only while the `GameController`'s `BattleUnits` is the campaign map's (`IsCampaign`, 78782), so a battle map's terrain is never probed or memoised; questions a review already memoised are passed over. A set larger than the memo (`MaxMemoEntries` 200000) is not drained (`campaign-planner:terrain-drain-oversize`) and the reviews fill the memo alone. The review keeps its all-or-nothing rule and its own budget as the fallback. **Segmented probes (verify r2 M2a, integrator decision):** a line is walked in segments sized to what the step and CPU budgets leave at the measured cost per step (`LineSegments.Steps`; the budget's first segment is at least `MinSegmentSteps` 32, so the memo always progresses), its offset kept in `Partials`; a segment starts where vanilla's own one-step walk is after the steps walked (`LineSegments.AlongLine`, GetAngleTerrainPosStatic's x/z, 26909-26912; the cell lookup reads only x and z, 26743-26744), so the segments sample the cells the single `CountTerrainLine` call would; the line is memoised when a segment meets a blocking cell or the last one ends. Reviews use the same segmentation within their 1 ms. **The memo belongs to the map (verify r2 M2b, integrator decision):** the memo, the partial lines and the drain survive `CampaignScope` resets (sidecar loads, returns from battle) and are cleared only when `TerrainMemoDrain.MapIdentity` (FNV-1a 64 over `SceneManagement.currentcampaignscenario` and every area's index and rounded centre) changes (`PrepareDrain`, before any probe of the review); tests `DrainMemoBelongsToTheMap`, `SegmentsStayWithinTheFrameBudget`, `SegmentStartIsVanillasWalk`. **Driver (done at the `main` 8099aaa merge, perf hot paths):** the drain is a client of the frame-budget daily-review scheduler: `MonthlyTickHookPatch` pumps `StrategicCoordinator.PumpDailyReview` on every live campaign frame without a date change, which continues an in-flight review day and, on a frame with no day in flight, runs `DrainTick` instead (never both in one frame; not from `Plugin.Update`); the review's 4 ms frame budget and the drain's 0.9 ms are separate (`TerrainDrainWiringPinned`, `PatchesPumpAndDrain`). The trace ends `terrain=complete` or `terrain=filling unknown=<n> probed=<m>`.
- **Battles:** `BattleInbox` (pure, in `PlannerState.cs`; `Take` drops battles older than `MaxBattleAgeDays` 7 at the review's day, so after a W&L pause the queue is not judged against today's main effort, verify r1 F7) queues each forwarded record for the alliances of 0 and 1 that fought it (`Alliance[]`, `AllianceWon`, `LosingAlliance`: the old R4 path ran for the winner and the loser only), at most 16 each; each `ReviewPlan` takes only its own queue, so one side's review never consumes the other's trigger and a CSA-European battle never moves the Union's main effort. The query uses `TheaterClassifier.FromPosition` for both theaters (pure, the classifier `SectorModel` uses) and the relocation range as radius, where the old `DirtyPlanIfBattleInvalidates` used `CampaignMapTheaterRuntime` and the catalog's near-target radius; G6 deletes that path.
- **Seed:** `PlannerState.ComputeCampaignSeed(SceneManagement.currentcampaignscenario, year, month, day)` at the first full review (`CampaignSeed == 0` = not yet), FNV-1a 64 over UTF-8 `scenario|yyyy-mm-dd`, folded to an `int` seed `(int)(s ^ s >> 32)` for `System.Random`.
- **Trace:** `out trace` (per candidate: score and terms, odds/required, eligibility; at most 12) is emitted as a `CampaignPlan:trace` Trace event when the `[CampaignPlan]` row changes, gated by `CicSettings.PlanTrace` (the existing Plan Trace Logging value; G6 moves it to `PlannerSettings` and rewrites the flag's description).
- **`[CampaignPlan]` row on change of its text** (`TelemetryRouter.LegacyInfo`, campaign sidecar): the switch reason is part of the row, so a decision row is followed by one `switch=hold` row on the next review. Reasons: `initial`, `hold`, `margin`, `capital-threat`, `accomplished`, `unavailable`, `odds`, `battle`, `no-candidate`, `none`.
- **Until G1 merges** `snapshot.Beliefs` is empty: every balance is 1, no capital threat, no Hold/Screen sectors. Nothing reads the plan before G5.
- **Depot tasks** are also laid when the main target is out of reach only because its zone is over vanilla's stacking cap (`OwnPoints > SupplyCapacity`, 10983; G0's `LogisticsGate.Evaluate`); recorded at verify r1, no change.

Census additions (rows below): `FormationRecord.GroupSpeed` (G3 `MarchTime.EstimateDays`); `RailLineRecord.Progress/AX..BZ/AIipId/BIipId` also G3 `MarchTime.RailLeg`; `FormationRecord.UseRivers` also G3 `MarchTime`; `PlannerState.Restore` (G6 sidecar; a name collision hides it from orphan-sweep's test-only list); `HistoricalPriorTable.Entries` (read by `PriorFor`).

Red-first: with API-only stubs (bodies returning defaults) 34 of the 42 tests failed, harness exit 134 on the first (`ReachFalseBeyondSourcesYieldsDepotTask`); the 8 that passed at red are regression guards that hold for a default answer (`SeededChoiceReproducible`, `AllianceTwoReturnsEmptyPlan`, `NullTempoIsNeutral`, `UnblockedLineKeepsStraightDistance`, the constants pin) and the three source pins of the runtime written alongside (`TerrainProbeMirrorsVanillaEngagementLine`, `PlannerPlanStepPinned`, `BattleForwardFollowsHistoryAdd`), proven by mutation below.

Mutation (scratch copy, one plant at a time, harness with a catching `RunAll` so every failing test is listed; `evidence/g3/mutation-results.txt`), every plant red: prior term `0.5 × prior` uncapped → `LiveMarginAbovePriorMaxWins`; challenger switches at `ChallengerDays >= 0` (`SwitchDays = 0`) → `HysteresisHoldsBelowMargin`; beliefs at full strength regardless of confidence (omniscient balance) → `UnseenEnemyDoesNotLowerBalance`; Recover on the halved supply → `RecoverUsesRawSupplyNotVanillaCap`; literal Screen reading → `ScreenOnlyBelowHoldNeedParityIsHold`; afloat beliefs in the land threat → `AfloatEnemyIsThreatNotHoldableContact`; capital trigger off → `CapitalThreatSwitchesImmediately`; tempo ignored → `TempoMassModifierRaisesRequiredRatio`; draw ignores `Epsilon` → `RandomOnlyWithinEpsilon`; detour through the first crossing → `RiverLineDetourViaNearestCrossing`; a blocked line with no crossing removes the threat → `NoCrossingNeverRemovesThreat`; train capacity ignored → `MarchTimeCreditsRailOnlyWithTrainCapacity`; existing depot not skipped → `DepotTasksSkipSiteNearAnExistingDepot`; inbox feeds alliance 0 only → `BattleInboxDeliversToBothSides`; inbox ignores who fought → `BattleInboxDeliversToBothSides`; runtime wiring: coordinator forward dropped → `BattleForwardFollowsHistoryAdd`; probe angle without `+ 180f` → `TerrainProbeMirrorsVanillaEngagementLine`; an empty plan published instead of the planner's → `PlannerPlanStepPinned`; one shared battle queue (`Take(0, …)`) → `PlannerPlanStepPinned`. Two plants first survived and the tests were tightened: the Epsilon plant (the third candidate sat so far below that the softmax never drew it; it now sits 0.06 below, where the softmax alone would draw it about once in forty, over 400 seeds) and the empty-plan plant (the pin checked only the call order; it now pins `Publish(alliance, plan, …)` and `EmitPlan(plan, trace, …)`).

Performance (net8 Release scratch benchmark over the pure files, `evidence/g3/bench/`): `TheaterPlanner.Plan` on a G0-landing-sized snapshot (300 own formations with area indexes, 60 areas, 120 objective targets, 450 supply sources, 50 complete rail lines, 40 beliefs, 30 crossings, a fixture probe) median 1.5-2.4 ms, measured with the host at load average 20-25 on 8 cores (parallel sessions), so the uncontended figure is lower; `AreaAt` is done once per candidate, distances compared squared, the own-IIP set built once per plan and the axis clamped once. The in-game cost of the vanilla probes is bounded by the step budget above; G7 smoke item 4 (`campaign.planner.plan` ≤ 2 ms, review ≤ 4 ms) remains the proof.

**Verify r1 (2026-10-15, integrator decisions F1-F9 on `379b471`).** F1 odds deadband (`OddsDeadbandHoldsTheMainEffort`, `OddsDropBelowDeadbandOrAfterSwitchDays`); F2 capital onset latch (`StandingCapitalThreatDoesNotFlap`, `CapitalLatchRearmsBelowRelease`); F3 order-independent, all-or-nothing river terms with deterministic probing (`RiverDiscountIsOrderIndependent`, `ReviewTerrainRecordsEachUnknownOnce`, budget refusal, first-probe rule and reset pinned in `TerrainProbeMirrorsVanillaEngagementLine`); F4 massable strength (balance term above, `EligibilityCountsMassableStrength`, `MassExcludesHoldsBusyAndMapGroupPlayer`, `MassOnlyWithinMarchHorizon`; interface `TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds`/`MassStrength`, `SectorPlan.HoldNeed`); F5 tests for the busy flags, seed parts, tie-break, unoccupied Screen and the other listed behaviours; F7 `StaleBattlesAfterAPauseAreDropped`; F8 the two record corrections above; F9 the G3 → G4 obligations in G4's section. Verify r2 (pass) landing: M1 pins (`CapitalLatchRearmsBelowRelease` keeps the latch at threat 0.5, `MassExcludesTheCapitalReserve`, a Recover formation outside the mass in `EligibilityCountsMassableStrength`), M3 (`PriorDecaysOutsideWindow` asserts 30.4375 on the calendar serial, 31 on the legacy one), M2a/M2b above; harness 2371 PASS. Round-1 record: harness 2367 PASS / 0 FAIL (2299 + 68 in `TheaterPlannerTests`; the drain adds `DrainEnumeratesExactlyTheAreaPairSet`, `DrainRespectsTheFrameBudget`, `DrainCompletesThenReviewAppliesRiverTerms`, `DrainAppendsNewCommandRangesOnly`, `TerrainDrainWiringPinned`); mutation plants all red in a scratch copy (`evidence/g3/mutation-results-final.txt`). New once-keys (not patches): `campaign-planner:terrain-drain-start`, `-complete`, `-oversize`, `-failed`.

Bench at verify r1 (net8 Release scratch over the pure files, `evidence/g3/bench/PlanBench2Tests.cs`; the G0-landing-sized snapshot above plus a terrain source modelled on `TerrainLineRuntime`, memo on the 16-unit grid, its three budgets and the first-probe rule, a busy-wait cost of 1 or 3 µs per `CountTerrainLine` step; 120 daily reviews, statistics over reviews 20-119; host load 10-12 on 8 cores): `Plan` mean 2.1-2.6 ms, p95 2.8-6.5 ms, max 7.1 ms after warm-up (29 ms on the JIT's first review); probing mean 0.5-0.9 ms, p95 ≤ 1.15 ms, max 1.6 ms. The plan step is bounded by the plan work plus the probe budget; at the loaded host's p95 it can exceed the 4 ms review, so G7 smoke item 4 (`campaign.planner.plan` ≤ 2 ms) remains the in-game proof. **The memo completes slowly:** the first review has 356-368 distinct questions; with static beliefs it completes on review 80 at 1 µs a step and needs about 350 reviews at 3 µs (one line a review); with beliefs moving 30 units a day on a quarter of the armies it does not complete within 120 reviews (new area pairs arrive about as fast as old ones are probed). Until it completes no candidate gets a river term (F3), so with the review budget alone river weighting would be absent for the first months of a campaign. The whole question set costs about 0.1-0.4 s of `CountTerrainLine` in total; the per-review budget, not the work, spread it over months. Integrator decision: enumerate the full static set and drain it per frame (above). Drain bench (`evidence/g3/bench/bench-drain-result.txt`, same snapshot, 60 areas): 1290 lines and 60 river points; at 1 µs a step 627 frames (about 10 s at 60 fps), frame mean 0.79 ms, p95 0.99 ms; at 3 µs 1206 frames (about 20 s), mean 1.22 ms, p95 1.80 ms (the first-probe rule lets one line of up to 600 steps run past the 1 ms target); one outlier frame each (5 and 13 ms) with the host loaded by a parallel mutation run; the review after the drain reads `terrain=complete`. With segmented probes and the 0.9 ms frame budget (verify r2, `bench/bench-drain-segmented-result.txt`): 524 frames at 1 µs, 1566 at 3 µs; probing per frame p50 0.90, p95 0.92 ms; frame p95 0.93 ms; the worst frame 5-7 ms in each run, a busy-wait preempted with the host loaded by a parallel plant run. The real map's set size and drain time are G7 smoke facts (the `terrain-drain-start`/`-complete` lines).

G3 smoke items (checked with G7's smoke; `LOG` and `SESSION` as there):
1. `SESSION/campaign*.jsonl`: `[CampaignPlan] alliance=1 main=<theater>/<objectiveId> ratio=<r> sectors=main:1,support:<n>,hold:<n>,eof:<n>,screen:<n>,recover:<n> switch=initial prior=<id|none> depots=<n>` on the first review, then rows only when the text changes (one `switch=hold` row after each decision); `switch=` only one of the reasons above; no more than one main-effort change per 3 days unless `switch=capital-threat|accomplished|unavailable|odds|battle`.
2. Before G1 merges: `main=` set with `hold:0,screen:0` (no beliefs). After G1: a sector facing a believed army shows in `hold:`/`screen:`.
3. With Plan Trace Logging on: `CampaignPlan:trace` events next to each changed row, `candidates` field listing `<objectiveId>@<sector> s=… v= b= r= m= e= c= p= odds=<own/enemy>/<required>`.
4. `SESSION/performance*.jsonl`: `campaign.planner.plan` meanMs ≤ 2 per alliance at 20x; `campaign.planner.review` within P2's 4 ms.
5. `LOG`: `[once:campaign-planner:terrain-drain-start] … lines=<n> rivers=<m>` after the first review of a load, then `[once:campaign-planner:terrain-drain-complete] … frames=<f>` (record `n`, `m`, `f`; never `-oversize` or `-failed`); `SESSION/performance*.jsonl` `campaign.planner.terrain-drain` meanMs ≤ 1.2 and no frame over 2 ms beyond one 600-step line; nothing from the drain during a battle. `[once:campaign-planner:terrain-probe-cap]` may appear once; `campaign.planner.plan` stays within item 4; never `campaign-planner:review-failed`. With Plan Trace Logging on, the trace ends `terrain=filling unknown=<n> probed=<m>` until the drain completes, then `terrain=complete` on every later review, also while beliefs move.
6. A decisive land battle at the main target followed by a `[CampaignPlan] … switch=battle` row on the next review when another candidate leads by more than 0.04; an out-of-supply main target shows `depots=1` or `2` and the main sector target short of the objective in the trace.
7. Verify r1: `switch=odds` only when the main effort's odds fall below the required ratio − 0.10 or stay below it for 3 reviews; no `switch=capital-threat` on consecutive reviews while one threat stands; `[Allocation]` (G4) masses the plan's `MassStrength`.

## G4 — ForceAllocator

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/ForceAllocator.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/HomeTheaterPrior.cs` (pure; data from `HistoricalArmyAreaRegistry`, which G6 deletes); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (step 5); test `ForceAllocatorTests.cs`.

**Vanilla to read:** `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` 14080-14158; `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` 11668-11869 (need, pruning 11751-11781); `UnitIsFightingForce` 16867; `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17232-17332 (what replacement flow can do); W&L: `GetCampaignGroup` 82991-83020, 8271, `IsMovedByPlayer` 45797.

**Interfaces — Consumes:** G0 `SectorModel`, `EnemyBeliefQuery`; G1b `ReconPlanner.PlanTasks` / `PickFormation` (the reason G4 merges after G1b; as built `PickFormation(s, sectors, plan, missions, in task, taken, mainEffortHasScreen)`, and the Recon assignment copies the task's `SectorId` and hold point into `SectorId`/`TargetX/Z` for `ReconMissionLedger.Sync`; see G1b as built); stage F `PlayerComplianceLedger.StrengthWeight`, `WlChainRules`; G3 (verify r1 F4/F9) `TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds`/`MassStrength` and `SectorPlan.HoldNeed`.

**G3 → G4 obligations (G3 verify r1, F4/F9):**
- **The main effort is exactly the planned mass.** G3 judged the main effort's odds on `TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds` (`TheaterPlanner.MassFor`), so G4 assigns MainEffort to exactly those formations: it takes Recon and DepotBuilder formations outside that set while one qualifies, and it never adds a formation G3 did not count. G3 built the set with G4's own CapitalGuard rule (nearest committed movable formations to the capital up to `CapitalFloorStrength`) and Hold withholding rule (per threatened sector: what holds there anyway, then its formations nearest the sector's believed army up to the hold need), so the two cannot diverge; test `MainEffortIsExactlyThePlannedMass`.
- **One threat function.** A sector's hold need is `SectorPlan.HoldNeed` as G3 published it: `(HoldRatio + Tempo.MinimumHoldRatioModifier) ×` the river-weighted, afloat-free believed land threat (`TheaterPlanner.SectorThreat`). G4 never recomputes it with `EnemyBeliefQuery.StrengthWithin` (straight distance, afloat included), or Hold/Screen and the hold need would diverge; `max(…, MinGarrisonStrength)` is applied on top of the published value. Test `HoldNeedIsThePlansHoldNeed`.
**Produces:**
```csharp
public static class ForceAllocator {
  public const float MinGarrisonStrength = 5000f; public const float UnreliableBelow = 0.5f; public const int NonComplianceDays = 5;
  public const float HomeTheaterTieBreak = 0.05f;
  // Allocation also lists Shortfalls (formations below their role's strength need, worst first) and Surpluses (Reserve /
  // EconomyOfForce strength above need) for G5b's replacement and recruit routing.
  public static Allocation Allocate(TheaterPlan plan, CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, float playerReliability01,
                                    int playerNonCompliantDays, System.Func<string, WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.Theater> homeTheater,
                                    System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<ReconTask> reconTasks); // from ReconPlanner.PlanTasks (G1b)
}
```
Rules: busy formations (in battle, retreat, raid, sea invasion, fort builder, vanilla depot builder, transfer, `Embarked`, and every formation whose `Chain` fails `WlChainRules.MayMove` except `PlayerMapGroup`) keep `Hold` in their current sector and are never moved; CapitalGuard first up to `plan.CapitalFloorStrength` (closest fighting formations; never a map-group player formation when the floor would depend on it alone); Hold sectors get `max(SectorPlan.HoldNeed, MinGarrisonStrength)` (the sector's **hold need**; `HoldNeed` is G3's `(HoldRatio + Tempo.MinimumHoldRatioModifier) ×` river-weighted believed land threat, never recomputed) from formations already there, then the nearest; **Hold withholding (spec component 6):** the sector's Hold formations are ordered by distance to the sector's nearest believed enemy (centroid when none), and the shortest prefix whose strength reaches the hold need gets `Withheld = true` (vanilla's offensive gather, 14257-14275, cannot take them); the rest are `Withheld = false` and vanilla may take them; Reserve, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon and Recover assignments are always `Withheld`; DepotBuilder one small formation per depot task (lowest strength fighting formation within the sector); Recover formations as assigned by the plan; then **Recon**: for each recon task in priority order, `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` (cavalry-majority first, never stripping the main effort's last cavalry screen, never a busy/player formation) gets `Recon` with the task's hold point and a mission id; MainEffort exactly `plan.MassInstanceIds` (above); the rest Support (support sectors) by distance with the home-theater tie-break; Reserve = what exceeds `RequiredRatio × believed enemy` at the main target by more than 30%. Player (F's chain classes): `CarryingGroup` (player below map level) → an ordinary movable formation (F's `MoveUnitTo` Postfix dispatches its marches); `PlayerMapGroup` → its strength counts × `PlayerComplianceLedger.StrengthWeight(true, playerReliability01)` in every strength sum, it is never the sole CapitalGuard or sole MainEffort holder (F's `CountsAsCommittedDefender` is false for it), and after `NonComplianceDays` its role is covered by another formation; `PlayerCommand` → busy (above); `CommanderInChief` class → empty allocation (stand down).

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first: `BusyFormationsKeepHold`, `PlayerCommandNeverAllocatedAMove`, `CapitalGuardFilledFirstUpToFloor`, `HoldSectorKeepsHoldRatio`, `EconomyOfForceKeepsMinimumGarrison`, `RemainderGoesToMainEffort`, `ReserveIsSurplusOverRequiredRatio`, `DepotBuilderIsSmallestFormationInSector`, `MapGroupPlayerDiscountedByReliability`, `MapGroupPlayerNeverSoleMustHold`, `MapGroupPlayerCoveredAfterNonComplianceDays`, `CarryingGroupIsMovable`, `HoldWithheldWhileSectorAtNeed` (the prefix that reaches the hold need is `Withheld`), `HoldReleasedAboveNeed` (Hold formations beyond that prefix are not), `AlwaysWithheldRoles`, `TempoHoldModifierRaisesHoldNeed`, `NullTempoIsNeutral` (G0 landing F6: `Tempo` is null on the first review), `CicPlayerYieldsEmptyAllocation`, `HomeTheaterBreaksTies`, `AllocationDeterministic`, `ReconAssignedBeforeSupportAndMainEffort`, `ReconPrefersCavalryFormations`, `ReconNeverStripsMainEffortScreen`, `ReconTaskWithoutFormationLeftUnassigned`, `AllocatorReportsShortfallAgainstRoleNeed`, `SurplusNeverFromMainEffortOrCapitalGuard`, `PlayerFormationNeverShortfallTarget`.
- [ ] Implement; scope `campaign.planner.allocate` (2.0). Telemetry `Allocation` on change: `[Allocation] alliance=<a> main=<n> support=<n> hold=<n> reserve=<n> capital=<n> depot=<n> recover=<n> recon=<n> busy=<n> player=<class> reliability=<r>`.
- [ ] Gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): force allocator [G4]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (`[Allocation]` rows; in a W&L corps career `player=MapGroup` and the player's corps never listed as the only `capital` holder).
