## G5a — Mover and steering executors (after G4 merges and after `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` merges)

**Depends on `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs`** (in progress on `main`): it fixes the zone supply-capacity freeze — `AIArea.iips/cbuildings/towns` are only filled while `airuns` ≤ 1 (14927, 14946, 14965), so depots and IIPs built later never count in `supplycapacity` (10772-10802) — and the importance-pointer redirect relies on vanilla's `ownpoints <= supplycapacity` gate (10983). G5a builds on that fix and does not re-fix it.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/MoveTracker.cs` (pure: issued/confirmed/failed/retry/give-up), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (pure view over the published allocation and the move tracker, read by the availability Postfixes), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignMoverRuntime.cs` (runtime), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSteeringRuntime.cs` (runtime: objective write, `theaterposition` writer), `Patches/CampaignAvailabilityFilterPatch.cs` (new; two classes); rewrite `Patches/ImportanceValuesPatch.cs` (#2); modify `Patches/PickCampaignObjectivePatch.cs` (transitional only: the executor-flag stand-down at the top of `Prefix`; its `SnapshotWrittenByMod`/`RestoreWrittenByMod` statics stay and the objective executor writes the mod-written value through them, so the coordinator's sidecar calls at 1911/1957 keep persisting it until G6 moves the field); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (executor step); test `CampaignExecutorTests.cs`.

**Vanilla to read:** `MoveUnitTo` 14479-14540 and the queue 9933, 9965-10052, 79295; `CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` 25608; `IsTerrainPositionRestricted` 25755; `unitsinoffensiveoperations` handling 14383-14385, `UpdateMicroMovementInOffensive` 13968-14061, `AdjustOffensiveOperationsSpeed` (11350), `CheckStanceSetting` 11893-11930; `CheckForEndOfDefensiveOperations` 13782-13848 (why never the defensive list); `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` 14080-14158 (Postfix target, public static); `IsUnitAvailableForDefensiveOperation` 13460 (private static, Postfix by name with the full parameter list); `CalculateMostValueableAIZones` 10964-11009 and the readers 14206-14228; `PickCampaignObjective` 17769-17789 and 14922/14961/12337; `UpdateCampaignTheaters` 17034-17110 (`theaterposition`, box 17090-17110); W&L: 8251-8275, 8366, 14381-14456 (order-instead-of-move); stage F as merged (`9aa71cf`; names unchanged since `c10aca3`): `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue(WlStrategicOrderRequest)` / `WlOrderTypeForIntent`, `WlCampaignDispatchRuntime.OnMoveUnitTo`/`Flush` (#69, the `MoveUnitTo` Postfix and its `UpdateUnitAI` flush), `WlCampaignDispatchPolicy.ClassifyType`, `WlMoveSource`.

**Interfaces — Consumes:** G0 store/snapshot/`SectorModel`, G4 `Allocation` (incl. `Assignment.Withheld`), G3 `TheaterPlan`; stage F (as merged at `9aa71cf`): `PlayerComplianceRuntime.Reliability` (→ `PlayerComplianceLedger.Reliability`), `WlChainRules.MayMove/OrderOnly`, and for a `PlayerMapGroup` formation `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue(new WlStrategicOrderRequest { AllianceId, Unit, TargetPosition, TargetName, Intent, SourceSystem = "campaign-planner" })`, which maps the intent to vanilla's order type (`WlOrderTypeForIntent`: `Offensive` 16, `Redeploy` 5, `DefendCapital` 8, `ConstructSupplyDepot` 10) and emits through `WlCampaignDispatchRuntime.EmitForPlayerCommand(Regiment, int, Vector3, string, float, float)` with F's dedupe. **Planner moves and F's typing:** the planner calls `MoveUnitTo` inside the daily review, outside `UpdateUnitAI`, where F's move source is `WlMoveSource.Other`; F's #69 Postfix records a `CarryingGroup` move and its next `UpdateUnitAI` flush types it by list membership (`ClassifyType`: order type 16 or 6 when listed in the offensive list, else order type 5), exactly as it types vanilla's own moves of that group. G5a therefore does **not** call `EnterMoveSource` (that hook names the vanilla callers list membership cannot classify, `RollUp` and `FinalPositions`) and adds no `WlMoveSource` value; it lists MainEffort/Support units in the offensive list right after `MoveUnitTo` returns `true`, before the flush.
**Produces:**
```csharp
public enum MoveOutcome { None = 0, Issued = 1, Confirmed = 2, Failed = 3, Retried = 4, GaveUp = 5, SkippedPlayer = 6, SkippedBusy = 7, Queued = 8 }
public sealed class MoveTracker { public const int MaxMovesPerDay = 6; public const float ConfirmFraction = 0.25f;
  public bool ShouldIssue(int instanceId, float tx, float tz, int daySerial);
  public void RecordIssued(int instanceId, float fromX, float fromZ, float tx, float tz, int daySerial, bool queued);
  public MoveOutcome Judge(int instanceId, float nowX, float nowZ, float waypointX, float waypointZ, int daySerial);
  public bool InTransit(int instanceId); /* issued or queued and not yet judged */
  public System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<MoveRecord> Records { get; } public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<MoveRecord> records); }
public struct MoveRecord { public int InstanceId; /* in-session */ public string UnitKey; /* persisted */ public float TargetX, TargetZ; public int IssuedDay; public int Retries; }
public static class CampaignWithholdView { // read by the availability Postfixes; G5c adds the depot-task hold, G8 the garrison role
  public static bool WithheldFromOffense(int alliance, int instanceId);                      // Assignment.Withheld || InTransit
  public static bool WithheldFromDefense(int alliance, int instanceId, float enemyX, float enemyZ); // as offense, except a Hold-at-need formation whose sector (SectorModel.SectorAt) contains the enemy position stays available
}
```

**Executors (inside the review, executor flag on):**
- **Objective (spec C6):** write `aifaction[f].followedcampaignobjective = plan.MainObjectiveId` when it differs and the objective is available; with no main effort, reset a mod-written value to −1 so vanilla re-picks (17780-17786). Records the mod-written value through `PickCampaignObjectivePatch`'s existing written-by-mod statics (still persisted by the coordinator at 1911/1957); G6 moves that state into the executor and deletes the patch.
- **Mover:** for each MainEffort/Support/CapitalGuard/Recover/DepotBuilder assignment whose formation is not where its role needs it: if its `Chain` fails `WlChainRules.MayMove`, the W&L rule below applies instead; otherwise validate the target (`CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` semantics; skip a restricted target), then `AICampaign.MoveUnitTo(unit, target, movearoundenemyunits: true)`; on `true` add MainEffort/Support units to `unitsinoffensiveoperations` (never `unitsindefensiveoperations`); `false` with a running order → `Queued`, retried next review. Judged next review (P8). W&L: a `PlayerCommand` chain class is never moved (`SkippedPlayer`); a `PlayerMapGroup` formation (`WlChainRules.OrderOnly`) gets `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue` with intent `Offensive` for MainEffort into enemy territory, `Redeploy` for other moves, `DefendCapital` for CapitalGuard, `ConstructSupplyDepot` for DepotBuilder (deduped by F); a `CarryingGroup` is moved normally and F's `MoveUnitTo` Postfix dispatches. Telemetry `[CampaignMover] alliance=<a> issued=<n> confirmed=<n> failed=<n> retried=<n> gaveUp=<n> queued=<n> skippedPlayer=<n>` once per review when any count > 0.
- **W&L player side transport modes (G0 landing B):** vanilla gives `userails = userivers = true` only to units of non-player alliances (`alliance != playeralliance`, 112862-112866); the player's alliance, including its AI-owned groups under `ai_vs_ai`, keeps stored values (field default false, 111466-111470; scenario/save data 74178-74180, 71375-71378). Before `MoveUnitTo` for a movable formation of the W&L player's alliance (`Chain` `None` or `CarryingGroup`) whose snapshot `UseRails`/`UseRivers` is false, the mover sets vanilla's AI default (`userails = userivers = true`); never on `PlayerCommand` or `PlayerMapGroup`. Tests `WlSideMovableGetsVanillaAiTransportDefault`, `PlayerCommandTransportFlagsUntouched`. Smoke: G0's first-review line for the player's alliance in a career shows `useRails=`/`useRivers=` (the real stored values); after G5a the same formations move along rivers and rails.
- **Theater box (spec C8):** for a MainEffort/Support formation already inside its sector, set `theaterposition` to the point toward its target that keeps the formation inside the box (`|unit − center| < theatersize` on both axes); for Hold formations, center on the formation. At most twice per formation per week.
- **Stale plans:** every planner patch and `CampaignWithholdView` ignore a published plan/allocation whose `DaySerial` is more than 2 review days older than today's (a paused alliance or a failing review never steers on stale state); test `StalePlanIgnoredByPatches`.
- **Importance pointers (#2 rewrite, spec C7):** Postfix on `AIArea.CalculateMostValueableAIZones(int aifaction)`: if `this` area lies in a MainEffort or Support sector of that faction's alliance (0/1, bound-checked), point **both** `mostvalueableaiareaclose[f]` and `mostvaluealbeaiareaclosedistanceonly[f]` at the area containing the sector target, only when that area passes vanilla's own gates from `this` (the distance and `ownpoints <= supplycapacity` checks of 10984; reuse `CampaignMilitaryGates.ShouldRedirectToPlanTarget`, re-keyed on the plan). Reads `CampaignPlanStore` only. First-fire id kept: `campaign.patch.importance-values`.
- **Recover = no overextension (finding: vanilla never checks supply before or during an offensive).** `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` (14080-14158) has no supply term and job 6's supply gate is dead (`flag` is always true, 14009-14019); job 6 re-targets listed units on morale/readiness only (14023-14030). So a Recover formation is (a) withheld by the availability Postfixes from new operations and (b) removed by the executor from `unitsinoffensiveoperations` and `unitsindefensiveoperations`, exactly as vanilla's `RelieveUnitsInOperations` (14068-14078) removes retreating/low-morale units, then moved by the mover toward its nearest supply source (`LogisticsGate`). Do not revive the dead vanilla gate (its 0.75 threshold is unreachable on the halved metric).
- **W&L per-unit decision:** the "may Whiskey move this group, or must it be an order" decision is the snapshot's `Chain` (F's `ChainClassOf`, G0) read through `WlChainRules.MayMove`/`OrderOnly`; `CarryingGroup → MayMove` is F's lifting of the old refusal for the group above a player below map level. G5a adds no classification of its own. Spec C4 stands.
- **Availability (new `CampaignAvailabilityFilterPatch`):** Postfix on `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations(int, Regiment, Vector3, bool, bool)` (14080, public static): `__result = false` when `CampaignWithholdView.WithheldFromOffense(alliance of the faction, unit)` (Reserve, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon, Recover, in-transit, and Hold at sector need). Postfix on `IsUnitAvailableForDefensiveOperation(int _aifaction, Regiment unit, Vector3 enemyposition)` (13460, private static, targeted by name with that parameter list): `__result = false` when `CampaignWithholdView.WithheldFromDefense(alliance, unit, enemyposition)`, which keeps a Hold-at-need formation available when `enemyposition` lies in its own sector (vanilla passes `enemyunits[j]`'s position at 13610, its unsorted-list bug, BUG-AI-002; the Postfix judges whatever position vanilla passed). Never sets `true`. First-fire ids `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive`, `campaign.patch.planner-availability.defensive`. Rollback: `Enable Campaign Planner Executors`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first (`CampaignExecutorTests`): `MoveCapRespectsMaxMovesPerDay`, `MoveConfirmedWhenWaypointChangesTowardTarget`, `MoveFailedThenRetriedThenGaveUp`, `QueuedMoveIsNotAFailure`, `PlayerCommandChainNeverMoved`, `MapGroupPlayerGetsOrderNotMove` (intent per role), `CarryingGroupMovedNormally`, `WithheldFromOffenseIsAssignmentWithheldOrTransit`, `HoldAtNeedAvailableForDefenseInOwnSector`, `HoldAtNeedWithheldFromDefenseOutsideSector`, `HoldAboveNeedNeverWithheld`, `MoveRecordsKeyedByUnitKey`, `RecoverRemovedFromOperationLists`, `TheaterBoxKeepsUnitInside`, `TheaterBoxRateLimited`, `ObjectiveWriteOnlyWhenChanged`, `ObjectiveResetOnlyWhenModWritten`, `ImportanceRedirectOnlyForMainOrSupportSectors`, `ImportanceRedirectRequiresVanillaGates`.
- [ ] Implement pure tracker + views; runtime executors; the patches with scopes `campaign.patch.importance-values`, `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive|defensive` (2.0).
- [ ] Until G6, the mover refuses formations `CoordinatedOperationRuntime.IsPackageLocked` reports and #1 is short-circuited by the objective executor's flag (P10): when `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` is on, `PickCampaignObjectivePatch.Prefix` returns `true` at once (G6 deletes the patch).
- [ ] Gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): mover, objective, theater and availability executors [G5a]`.

## G5b — Capital floor, replacement flow, career transfer fix (after G4 merges)

**Owned files:** rewrite `Patches/DefensiveOpsPatch.cs` (#4 → capital floor; reads the allocation's CapitalGuard choice and beliefs; keeps stage F's chain-class guard: only formations `WlChainRules.MayMove` allows are pledged), rewrite `Patches/TransferOfUnitsPatch.cs` (#3 → reads the allocation's shortfalls/surpluses and beliefs; the omniscient `recruitmentheatmapenemy` read at `:126-129` goes, and the career `transfer-queued` log is already truthful on `main` `742d7e4` (the logistics fix reports `transfer-queued` only when `SetTransfer` started a transit, `TransferOfUnitsPatch.cs:386-395`; nothing to gate)), create `Patches/RecruitRoutingPatch.cs` (new), `Strategic/Planner/ReinforcementRouting.cs` (pure), create `Patches/WlCareerTransferFixPatch.cs` (new, two classes), `Strategic/Planner/CapitalFloorPolicy.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/ReplacementFlowPolicy.cs` (pure); modify `Strategic/CampaignMilitaryGates.cs` (capital/transfer helpers re-keyed on the plan; `TransferHeatmapFields` deleted), `Patches/RecruitmentPatch.cs` (an `internal static bool InZoneRecruiting` and `internal static int ActiveAifaction` accessor over the existing `[ThreadStatic] _zoneDepth`/`_activeAifaction`, `:16-17`; nothing else in that file); test `CapitalAndReplacementTests.cs`. Plugin: bind `Enable W&L Career Transfer Fix`.

**Vanilla to read:** `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` 11668-11869 (need 11712-11741; prune 11751-11781; add 11791-11817; move 11819-11867); `ZoneRecruiting` 12405 / heatmap deficit 12467-12520; `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17232-17332 (17327-17328); `BattleUnits.MoveUnit` 93091-93150 (guard 93099; `SetTransfer` 93147); `GrabExistingGroup` 12739-12806 (callers: `ZoneRecruiting` 12558, `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17319; `RemovePlayerUnits` 12755/12790/12797; may create a division in a corps, 12796-12801); `RaiseNewCampaignGroup` 12940-13076; recruit travel `SetTransfer` 139051-139075 (`speedforunittransfers` 45/yr, campaignprefs 269-270); `CheckCombinationOfUnits` 17158 (`skipdlccheck: true` precedent).

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public static class CapitalFloorPolicy {
  // 11712-11741 with beliefs in place of enemyunits: minStrength + Σ min(str, str×morale)/max(10,d)^0.3×0.5 over beliefs within range − own str×morale closer than the nearest enemy
  public static float VanillaNeed(float minStrengthForMovement, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> beliefs, float believedMorale,
                                  float capitalX, float capitalZ, float relocationRange, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<FormationRecord> own);
  public static int PickPledge(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, float vanillaNeed); /* instanceId or -1 */ }
public static class ReplacementFlowPolicy { // planShortfall = required strength − own strength at the main target; believedEnemy = EnemyBeliefQuery.StrengthWithin there. No vanilla heatmap enemy term.
  public static bool ShouldSteer(TheaterPlan plan, float believedEnemyAtTarget, float planShortfall, out float targetX, out float targetZ); }
public static class ReinforcementRouting { public const float MaxRouteFactor = 1.5f; // × relocation range from the recruiting cell
  public static bool TryRouteRecruit(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, float cellX, float cellZ, out float routeX, out float routeZ); // nearest shortfall formation
  public static bool TryTransferPair(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, out float deficitX, out float deficitZ, out float surplusX, out float surplusZ); }
```

**#4 rewrite (Prefix, Priority.High, non-skip; unchanged rule):** pre-pledge the allocation's nearest CapitalGuard formation into `groupstodefendcapital` only while vanilla's own need (`num2`, recomputed by `CapitalFloorPolicy.VanillaNeed` from beliefs, the 11712-11741 formula) is > 0 and the formation passes vanilla's 11791 filters; never a player-owned or custom-ordered (`CoastalDefenseCustomOrderRunner.IsCustomOrdered`) unit. The excess floor stays CapitalGuard (G5a mover + availability). First-fire id kept `campaign.patch.defensive-ops`. Telemetry `[CapitalFloor] alliance=<a> vanillaNeed=<x> floor=<y> pledged=<unit|none>` on change.

**#3 rewrite (Prefix/Postfix/Finalizer, snapshot-restore, unchanged mechanism):** swap `positiondeficit` for the worst shortfall formation's position and `positionsurplus` for the largest surplus formation's position when `ReinforcementRouting.TryTransferPair` succeeds (else the main-effort target via `ReplacementFlowPolicy.ShouldSteer`, with the plan shortfall and the believed enemy at the target; vanilla's `positiondeficit.enemystrength`, the old `:142` read, is no longer read); vanilla then moves one brigade from near the surplus to a division near the deficit (`GrabExistingGroup` 17319, `MoveUnit` 17328) — in careers through the C1 fix. Finalizer restores. Ids kept `campaign.patch.transfer-of-units.prefix|postfix|finalizer`.

**Recruit routing (new `RecruitRoutingPatch`):** vanilla attaches each new regiment to a division near the recruiting heatmap cell (`GrabExistingGroup` from `ZoneRecruiting` 12558) and nobody routes it to an army that needs it. Vanilla's target is `private static Regiment GrabExistingGroup(AIFaction _aifaction, Vector3 position, float limitdivisions = 1f, float searchradiusfactor = 1f, bool enhanceradius = true)` (12739; `AIFaction` is a private nested type, 10299, and `position` is passed by value): the patch targets it by name through `AccessTools.Method(typeof(AICampaign), "GrabExistingGroup")` and declares only `static void Prefix(ref Vector3 position)` (Harmony's by-ref argument edit; no `AIFaction` parameter). Argument edit, no skip. It acts only while `RecruitmentPatch.InZoneRecruiting` is true (the existing `ZoneRecruiting` scope, so the transfer call at 17319 is untouched) and takes the faction from `RecruitmentPatch.ActiveAifaction`; replaces `position` with the nearest shortfall formation within `MaxRouteFactor ×` relocation range of the cell (`ReinforcementRouting.TryRouteRecruit`); vanilla's own division search, `RemovePlayerUnits` and abstract travel (`SetTransfer`) then run unchanged. First-fire `campaign.patch.recruit-routing` (situational: only when vanilla recruits). Telemetry `ReplacementFlow`: `[ReplacementFlow] alliance=<a> kind=<recruit|transfer> to=<formation> from=<cell|formation>`.

**Career transfer fix (C1, decided yes; BUG-WL-013):** `WlCareerTransferScopePatch` Prefix/Finalizer on `CheckTransferOfUnits` sets/clears a `[ThreadStatic]` scope when `DLC_WL.dlc_scenarioactive`; `WlCareerTransferMoveUnitPatch` Prefix on the instance method `BattleUnits.MoveUnit(GameObject unittomove, GameObject unittomovebelow, bool skipdlccheck = false)` (93091; the flag is passed by value, so the Prefix declares `ref bool skipdlccheck` as Harmony's argument edit) sets `skipdlccheck = true` while the scope is set **and neither unit is `dlcw_isundercommander`** (never re-parents a player unit). No skip; vanilla's body runs. First-fire ids `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.scope`, `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.move-unit` (situational: careers only). `OnceLog.Info("campaign-planner:wl-transfer-fix-first", "[WlTransferFix] career transfer allowed unit=<name> to=<name>")`.

## G5c — Construction steering: depots, forts, railroads (after G5a merges and after `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` merges)

**Depends on `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs`**, which is fixing vanilla job 19's two bad depot branches on `main` now: (a) an unguarded **enemy** depot admitted as a supply source (`own | allied | !EnemyUnitsInRange`, 14740) and (b) a unit skipped entirely when any own-started depot (built or not) is within command range (14683-14715) — "starving next to an empty depot". G5c builds on that fix and does not re-fix them. Supply depots, forts and telegraph stations have `subsidytype -1` (`buildingtypes.dat` 353, 1094, 1137), so `Place` charges the treasury (`constructioncosts`, 96211-96257), never a subsidy lane (see `docs/findings.md` § subsidy lanes).

**Dead construction-ledger code on `main` this slice wires (no dead code left):** `ConstructionRuntime` never creates a `ConstructionCandidateKind.SupplyDepot` candidate (it creates PrivateBuilding `:133`, Railroad `:172`, Fort `:204`, and telegraph candidates), so `TopSupplyDepot` and the depot clause of `IsEmergencyAllowed` (`ConstructionIntentLedger.cs:139-140`) are dead; `TopRailroad`, `TopFort` and the CSA rail cap (`ConstructionSuppressionReason.CsaRailDoctrineCap`, `:112-115`) are read only by telemetry (`StrategicCoordinator.cs:1532-1535`) because `ConstructionSteeringScorer.SuppressionFor` (`:45`) serves #20's private-building scaling and skips other kinds. Wiring:
- `ConstructionRuntime.BuildInput` creates one `SupplyDepot` candidate per `TheaterPlan.DepotTasks` entry (read from `CampaignPlanStore`); `DepotTaskLedger.Sync` starts a task only when the ledger did not suppress its candidate (fiscal posture), with the existing emergency allowance letting depots through `EmergencyHold`. `TopSupplyDepot` is the next task to start.
- `RailLineScorer` multiplies by the ledger's Railroad candidate score for that line and excludes a line the ledger suppressed (CSA rail cap); `TopRailroad` breaks score ties.
- The fort preference ranks the ledger's Fort candidates; `TopFort` is the preferred site when it is eligible.
- `SuppressionFor` stays private-building-only (its #20 purpose), with a comment naming the G5c consumers of the other kinds.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/DepotTaskLedger.cs` (pure: Marching → Wished → Built | Abandoned), `Strategic/Planner/DepotSteeringRuntime.cs` (runtime), `Strategic/Planner/RailLineScorer.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/RailLineSteering.cs` (pure: the roll loop below), `Patches/RailroadLineSteeringPatch.cs` (new); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (G5a's file: a builder `DepotTaskLedger.HoldsBuilder` holds stays withheld from both pools); modify `Patches/FortConstructionGovernorPatch.cs` (axis site preference; enemy read already on beliefs from G1), `Strategic/Construction/FortConstructionGovernor.cs` (pure site-preference rule), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionRuntime.cs` (depot candidates), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionIntentLedger.cs` (only if a signature change is needed), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionSteeringScorer.cs` (comment); test `ConstructionSteeringTests.cs`. Plugin: bind `Enable Supply Depot Steering`, `Enable Fort Steering`, `Enable Railroad Steering`.

**Vanilla to read:** `CBuilding.AddConstructionWish` 97479-97536; `Place` 96163-96257 (cost); `WorkDownConstructionWishes` 97538-97591; `IncreaseCondition` 96607-96738 (builder in bugle range 96626-96655); building ids 95083-95121; `CheckSupplyDepotConstruction` 14659-14779; `CheckFortConstruction` 16347-16405, `FortConstructionOrder` 9636-9675, 9814-9865, `fortconstructionsites` 81931-81943; `UpdateRailroadConstruction` 16052-16074; `Railroad.StartConstruction` 77818-77845; `UpdateConstruction` 77859-77893.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public enum DepotTaskState { Marching = 0, Wished = 1, Built = 2, Abandoned = 3 }
public struct DepotTaskRecord { public float X, Z; public int SectorId; public int BuilderInstanceId; /* in-session */ public string BuilderUnitKey; /* persisted */ public DepotTaskState State; public int StartedDay; }
public sealed class DepotTaskLedger { public const int TimeoutDays = 20; public const int MaxActivePerAlliance = 1;
  public void Sync(TheaterPlan plan, Allocation a, int daySerial);
  public bool ShouldWish(int taskIndex, float builderX, float builderZ, float bugleRange, int daySerial);
  public void RecordWished(int taskIndex, int daySerial); public void RecordBuilt(int taskIndex); public void Abandon(int taskIndex, string reason);
  public bool HoldsBuilder(int instanceId);
  public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<DepotTaskRecord> Tasks { get; }
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<DepotTaskRecord> tasks); }
public static class RailLineScorer { public static float Score(in RailLineRecord r, CampaignSnapshot s, TheaterPlan plan, SectorModel sectors); } // by the line's endpoints (G0: `AX..BZ`, `AIipId/BIipId`), not its average point: an endpoint that is an own supply source (`s.Supply` by `Id`) and an endpoint near the main axis or a threatened sector score; main-effort supply reach gain
public static class RailLineSteering { // pure replica of vanilla's loop with the steering choice
  // eligible[j] = progress[j] <= 0 && startable[j] (StartConstruction(a, checkonly: true)), taken once before any start.
  // rolls[j] = the j-th Random.Range(0f, 1f) draw (one per line, in line order, as 16069 draws before testing progress).
  // Returns the started line indices (one per success on an eligible line; none for a success on an ineligible line).
  public static int[] Choose(bool[] eligible, float[] rolls, float probabilityPerCall, float[] score); }
```
Depot executor: the DepotBuilder (G4) marches (G5a mover); on arrival within its `buglerange` of the task site, `CBuilding.AddConstructionWish(CBuilding.id_supplydepot, site, nearestOwnIip, alliance)` once (P8), skipped when the IIP already has `currentlyunderconstruction` (97479); `Place` charges the treasury with no funds check (`AdjustTreasury`, 96211-96257; depots have `subsidytype -1`), so affordability is decided before the task starts, by the construction ledger's fiscal suppression above; the builder stays withheld (`CampaignWithholdView`, via `DepotTaskLedger.HoldsBuilder`) until the depot's `constructiontimer <= 0` (`Built`) or `TimeoutDays` (`Abandoned`). Telemetry `[DepotTask] alliance=<a> site=<x>,<z> builder=<unit> state=<Marching|Wished|Built|Abandoned> reason=<…>`. Vanilla's job 19 is otherwise unchanged (its two bug branches are the bug-fix slice's).
Fort steering: #27's filter additionally hides, for the call, `fortconstructionsites` farther than `rangefortconstructionsite` from every MainEffort axis / Hold-or-Screen sector boundary **when at least one axis/threatened site is otherwise eligible** (snapshot-restore as today); vanilla's one-order-per-alliance rule and builder logic are untouched.
Railroad steering (C11 option a, approved by the user 2026-10-15): Prefix (bool) on `UpdateRailroadConstruction(int alliance, float timediff)`. It lets vanilla run (result `true`) for the player's alliance outside `ai_vs_ai`, for alliance ids of 2 and above, for the W&L CIC player's alliance, while vanilla's job machine is paused (`ExecutorsPaused`, 11368: vanilla keeps rolling railroads on the alliance tick, so its own roll runs), with no published plan, or with the flag off. Otherwise: (1) compute vanilla's probability `num` exactly as 16058-16066; (2) before any start, take `eligible[j] = constructionprogress ≤ 0 && StartConstruction(alliance, checkonly: true)` for every line (vanilla starts only the line it is rolling, so at line `j`'s roll the state of `j` equals the state at entry; 77818-77831 refuses a complete line, unowned IIPs and a non-permitted line); (3) draw `Random.Range(0f, 1f)` once per line in line order, as 16069 does before it tests progress; (4) a success on an ineligible line starts nothing, as in vanilla; a success on an eligible line starts the highest-scored eligible line not yet started in this call (line `j` itself when none scores higher) with `StartConstruction(alliance)`; (5) skip vanilla. So every success on an eligible line starts exactly one line, every started line is one vanilla could start, and the number and timing of starts equal vanilla's for the same draws (`RailLineSteering.Choose` holds the decision; the patch only reads state and calls `StartConstruction`). Header comment carries the spec's written justification. First-fire `campaign.patch.railroad-steering`. Telemetry `[RailroadSteer] alliance=<a> rolled=<j> started=<k> score=<s>`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first: `DepotWishOnlyOnArrivalWithinBugleRange`, `DepotWishOncePerTask`, `DepotBuilderHeldUntilBuilt`, `DepotAbandonedAfterTimeout`, `OneActiveDepotTaskPerAlliance`, `DepotBuilderWithheldWhileHeld`, `DepotTasksKeyedByBuilderUnitKey`, `FortPreferenceOnlyWhenAxisSiteEligible`, `FortPreferenceKeepsVanillaSitesOtherwise`, `RailScorePrefersMainAxis`, `RailScoreUsesEndpointsNotAverage` (two lines with one average point: the one linking own supply to the main axis wins), `RailSteeringPreservesSuccessCount` (`RailLineSteering.Choose` against a pure replica of vanilla's 16067-16073 loop over the same seeded draws; the fixture includes a line with unowned IIPs, a non-permitted line, a line already under construction and a complete line, all ineligible, plus a case where the best line is started before its own index is rolled and then rolls a success itself; the start counts are equal in every seed), `RailSuccessOnIneligibleLineStartsNothing`, `RailEachLineStartedAtMostOncePerCall`, `RailSteeringStandsDownForPlayerAndEurope`, `RailSteeringStandsDownWhileJobMachinePaused`, `DepotCandidateCreatedFromPlanDepotTask`, `DepotTaskSuppressedByFiscalPostureExceptEmergency`, `CsaRailCapExcludesLineFromSteering`, `TopRailroadBreaksScoreTies`, `TopFortPreferredWhenEligible`.
- [ ] Implement; gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): depot, fort and railroad steering [G5c]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (`[DepotTask]` Marching → Wished → Built for one task; `[RailroadSteer]` rows only for AI alliances; #27 `[Patch:FortGovernor]` lines unchanged in shape).

## G5d — Reconnaissance executor (after G5a merges)

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/ReconExecutorRuntime.cs` (runtime), consume `Strategic/Planner/ReconOrders.cs` (built in G1b's pure half with its tests, see G1b as built; pure `internal static class`, because its methods take F's internal `WlChainClass`: `CavalryOrderFor(ReconKind kind, ReconState state, float cavalryShare, WlChainClass chain)` returning the `ChangeCampaignOrders` code 121 (scouting) or 120 (no cavalry order), or −1 for no call; `MayAct(WlChainClass chain)`); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (executor step after G5a's mover), `Strategic/OperationalProbeRuntime.cs` (transitional: `Run` returns at once while the recon executor is active, so the old probe never moves a formation beside the new probe missions; G6 deletes the file); test additions in `ReconPlannerTests.cs` for the pure helpers it adds (`ReconOrders.CavalryOrderFor`, `ReconOrders.MayAct`).

**Vanilla to read:** `MoveUnitTo` 14479-14540; `ChangeCampaignOrders` 90720-90770 (it returns at once when `cavalryorders` already equals the new order, 90738, so a repeat call is free) and the queued apply 125409-125412; `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel` 92572-92620 (level −1 reaches the group and every descendant three levels down); `SetCavalryOrders` 122074-122082; `UpdateUnitStatusCampaign` 119914-119935; reinforcement delay 67742-67744; readiness reset 113960; W&L 8271, 8366, 14381-14456; stage F's dispatcher and `MoveUnitTo` Postfix.

**Executor (inside `ReviewExecute`, `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` and `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance` on):**
- Moves go through G5a's `MoveTracker` (same confirm/retry/give-up rules, P8) with a separate budget `MaxReconMovesPerDay 3` so recon never starves the main effort; target validation as G5a (`CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` semantics).
- A cavalry-majority screen gets cavalry order 1 (Scouting) — something vanilla's AI never does — through the player's own order path, `bunits.ChangeCampaignOrders(reg, 121)` (90720-90770: immediate `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel(…, -1, "SetCavalryOrders", 1)` at 90758 without order delays, else queued and applied at 125409-125412), and `120` when recalled or cleared (`ReconOrders.CavalryOrderFor`). **Never on a group that carries the W&L player:** level −1 sends `SetCavalryOrders` to the group and every descendant (92572-92620), so a `CarryingGroup` screen would put order 1 on the player's own brigade or regiments, which also delays their arrival as battle reinforcements (orders 1 and 3, 67742-67744). Setting the order on individual non-player regiments instead would not work: the scouting intel term tests the **group's** own `cavalryorders` (120424-120426) and the "Scouting" status is the group's (119923). So `CavalryOrderFor` returns −1 (no call) for `CarryingGroup`: that screen moves and views like any other, without the order. Effects are vanilla's: "Scouting" status (119923), intel → readiness (120424-120426, 115017/115306), scouting perk experience (115368-115371), later arrival as a battle reinforcement (67742-67744). It does not widen sight; position does. Pickets and advance guards get no cavalry order.
- Recalled missions move back to the hold point the ledger gives; contact is judged from beliefs and `inbattle` only (never from `closestenemyunitfarreg` or enemy destinations, which vanilla's raid abort uses, 9099). Moves are plain `MoveUnitTo(…, true)` (no forced march; screens halt at night like any group, 133958-133961).
- W&L: never the player's own command (`ReconOrders.MayAct` refuses the `PlayerCommand` chain class, re-read live with `ChainClassOf` before acting); a `CarryingGroup` used as a screen is moved normally, F's `MoveUnitTo` Postfix dispatches, and it gets no cavalry order; a `PlayerMapGroup` formation (never the sole screen) gets `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue` with intent `Redeploy` (type 5) to the hold point, no cavalry order; works for the player's AI side under `ai_vs_ai` (its detection is `OwnSideWithinFog`).
- **#15 stand-down (from G1b as built, item 15):** a formation on a recon mission gets directive Screen, which `FormationDirectiveRuntime.ShouldAllowAreaMovement` still allows, so #15 (`ArmyAreaTheaterPatch`, deleted in G6) could move a screen off its post. Until G6, a formation with an active recon mission is refused #15's area moves (the P10 stand-down: `ShouldAllowAreaMovement` returns false for `FormationDirective.Screen`, or #15 checks `ReconMissionLedger.IsRecon`); test `ReconFormationStandsDownFromAreaMoves`. G6 removes the branch with #15.
- Telemetry `ReconMission` per transition: `[ReconMission] alliance=<a> unit=<name> kind=<Screen|Picket|AdvanceGuard|Probe> state=<Moving|Holding|Recalled|Cleared> reason=<…>` (signature-deduped).

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first: `ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens`, `RecallClearsScoutingOrder`, `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand` (`CavalryOrderFor` is −1 for `CarryingGroup`, `PlayerMapGroup` and `PlayerCommand` in every kind and state), `NoScoutingOrderOnGroupContainingPlayerCommand` (the executor issues no `ChangeCampaignOrders` call for a `CarryingGroup` screen and still moves it), `ReconBudgetSeparateFromMainEffort` (MoveTracker with two budgets), `PlayerCommandRefusedLive` (pure decision `ReconOrders.MayAct(chain)`), `ReconFormationStandsDownFromAreaMoves` (#15 stand-down until G6). (`ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens`, `RecallClearsScoutingOrder`, `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand` and `PlayerCommandRefusedLive` were written with `ReconOrders` in G1b's pure half.)
- [ ] Implement; scope `campaign.planner.recon-execute` (2.0); gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): reconnaissance executor [G5d]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (smoke items 1.10-1.12 and Session B/C recon items).

**Sidecar v3 (`PersistenceDto.cs`, `SidecarDtoCodec.cs`):**
- `SidecarDto.CurrentVersion = 3`; readers accept 1..3. History comment: `3 = campaign planner: plan state, beliefs, task ledgers; CIC plans dropped`.

Rebinding on the first review after a load: `CampaignSnapshot.IndexOfKey(unitKey)` (own formations; for beliefs, the next detection under that key), falling back to the unit name alone when the commander part no longer matches; an unresolved own-unit record (move, mission, depot task, garrison post) is dropped, and a belief keeps decaying under its key until it is seen again or expires.
- `SidecarDto` loses `minorOfficerProfiles` (dead state).
- **Migration from v1/v2:** Newtonsoft ignores the removed members, so a v1/v2 file decodes with no planner state: plans dropped, beliefs empty (rebuilt on the next review), hysteresis fresh, `campaignSeed` computed at the first review after the load (P5). `PlanFromDto`/`MatchesCatalog` are deleted with the catalog; `InferModWritten` keeps working off `modWrittenObjective` and the live `followedcampaignobjective`. Load logs `[Coordinator] sidecar v<n> migrated to v3: plans dropped=<n>` once.
- Stage E adds need/policy fields to `FactionDto` in parallel; they are additive and version-agnostic — keep them when rebasing.
- Tests (`SidecarMigrationTests` in `CampaignLifecycleFixTests.cs` or a new `PlannerSidecarTests.cs`): `V2SidecarWithActivePlanLoadsWithoutPlan`, `V1SidecarLoads`, `V3RoundTripsPlannerState`, `V3RoundTripsBeliefsAndTasks`, `V3RoundTripsReconMissions`, `V3ReloadReboundsBeliefsByStableKey` (a v3 file restored under new instance ids: beliefs match the next detections by `unitKey`, no duplicate, `StrengthWithin` unchanged), `V3ReloadKeepsReconMissionFormation` (a mission's formation is found again by `IndexOfKey` after the reload; smoke 1.9), `V3DropsUnresolvedOwnUnitRecords`, `NoDtoCarriesInstanceId` (source pin over `PersistenceDto.cs`), `FutureVersionRejected`, `MinorOfficerProfilesNoLongerWritten`.

## G7 — Integration, read census, docs, smoke (integrator, last)

**Owned files:** `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/PlannerReadCensusTests.cs` (new), `docs/*`, `AGENTS.md` (locked choices #3-#6 rewritten per the spec; architecture paragraph; campaign write owners), `MEMORY.md`, `docs/patch-catalog.md` (ordinals for new patches in merge order; removed rows for #1, #15, #38; rewritten rows #2, #3, #4, #6, #27; runtime rows for `CampaignPlannerRuntime`/`CampaignMoverRuntime`/`DepotSteeringRuntime`/`CommanderAppointmentRuntime`; `WlStrategicOrderBridge` row as F leaves it), `docs/handoff.md`, `docs/telemetry.md` (new events), `docs/historical-operation-doctrine.md` (prior semantics), `docs/strategic-recon-commitment.md` (beliefs shipped), `docs/wl-dispatch-objective-bridge.md` + `docs/wl-player-order-doctrine.md` (planner invariants: map-group player = order-only, below-map-level parent movable, CIC stand-down), `docs/bug-fixes/README.md` (BUG-WL-013, BUG-ECO-004/005 status), `tools/smoke-situational.txt`, the test csproj (final reconciliation).

- [ ] Wiring checks: every review runs snapshot → sectors → detect → beliefs → profiles → plan (with logistics and depot tasks) → recon tasks → allocation → recon mission sync → publish, then appointments → executors (G5b, G5a, G5c, G5d) in that order (`CampaignPlannerRuntime` source pin test `PipelineOrderPinned` in `CampaignExecutorTests`); every planner patch reads only `CampaignPlanStore`/`CampaignWithholdView`.
- [ ] **Read census (no written-never-read members; `orphan-sweep` counts a write as a reference, so it cannot prove this).** For every G0 contract member and every `[Campaign Planner]` flag, the census below names the slice and method that **reads** it. G7 proves each row with a read-only grep **scoped to the reader file(s) the row names** (generic names such as `X`, `Z`, `Id`, `Index`, `Name`, `Available` or `Strength` would otherwise hit unrelated types): `rg -n "\.<Member>\b" <reader file>` (e.g. `rg -n "\.Supply01\b" src/WhiskeyRealism/Strategic/Planner/TheaterPlanner.cs`) must show at least one hit in that file that is not an assignment (`\.<Member>\s*=[^=]` excluded), pasted per row into the report; a member with no such hit is deleted before the commit, never kept for a later stage. A test file `PlannerReadCensusTests.cs` (G7) pins the list: it parses `CampaignPlannerContracts.cs`/`CampaignSnapshot.cs` for public members and fails when a member is missing from the census table it carries, so a member added later without a reader row turns the harness red.

   5. `[Allocation] alliance=1 …` with `main > 0` and `capital > 0`; `[CampaignMover] alliance=1 issued=<n> confirmed=<n> …` with `confirmed/issued ≥ 0.5` over the session and `gaveUp` small.

   7. Performance (`SESSION/performance*.jsonl`, run with `Logging Profile = FullTuning`: under the default `TacticalTuning` the planner scopes are not timed): every window's `maxMs` per alliance at 20x, including the first review after a load, ≤ 2 for `campaign.planner.snapshot`, ≤ 4 for `campaign.planner.review` (P2: over budget is a defect, there is no deferral), ≤ 1 for `campaign.planner.detect` and `campaign.planner.recon`, and `slowCount` 0 for each; `campaign.daily-review` and `campaign.patch.monthly-tick-hook` `maxMs` not worse than the pre-G0 baseline by more than 3 ms. Means are reported, not gated.

Save, reload: `[Coordinator] sidecar loaded`, the next `[CampaignPlan]` keeps the same `main=` (hysteresis state persisted), `[Recon]` keeps its missions on the same formations (rebound by `unitKey`), and `[EnemyBeliefs] remembered=` does not jump on the first review after the load (no double counting).

Recon: `[Recon] alliance=1 screens=<n ≥ 1> pickets=<n> advanceGuards=<n> probes=<n> recalled=<n> unwatchedApproaches=<n> oldestBeliefDays=<n>`; `[ReconMission] alliance=1 unit=<name> kind=Screen state=Moving` then `state=Holding`; a cavalry screen shows "Scouting" in its campaign status (vanilla text, 119923).

Recall: a screen that meets an enemy logs `[ReconMission] … state=Recalled reason=contact` and moves back, and one whose raw supply drops below 0.5 logs `reason=supply`; no screen sits inside an enemy army's bugle range for more than one review; the main-effort sector never loses its last cavalry screen (`[Allocation]` + `[Recon]`).

If the player's carrying cavalry group is used as a screen: it moves, F's dispatch panel appears, it shows no "Scouting" status and the player's own brigade keeps `cavalryorders` 0 (no Whiskey cavalry order reaches the player's units).

## G8 — Garrison manning beyond the capital (after G7)

Why: vanilla defends only the capital. `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` (11668-11869) is the only proactive defense; `CheckOccupationOfLostCities` (job 29, 17392-17485) is reactive — it sends the closest available unit to retake a lost own-state town that no enemy currently holds, if zone supply allows. No `AICampaign` code creates or mans garrisons (no `isgarrison: true` / `garrisonreference =` write in 8775-17933): garrisons exist only from the scenario (81991-81993) and as the dummy a finished fort spawns (`CreateGarrisonDummy` 82081-82093, `basicgarrisonsize[level]`; 96676-96686); `SeparateUnits` keeps every garrison out of `ownunits` (10510-10513), and the capital need's garrison loop is dead (11742-11750). Key towns, rail hubs, ports, depots and forts away from the capital are therefore held only by whatever happens to stand there.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonPlanner.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonLedger.cs` (pure, persisted), `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonRuntime.cs` (runtime executor); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`FormationRole.Garrison = 8`, `GarrisonPost` struct), `Strategic/Planner/ForceAllocator.cs` (Garrison step), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (Garrison withheld from both pools; the allocator sets `Withheld` for the Garrison role), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (forts with their garrison dummy strength, own depots), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs`, `PersistenceDto.cs` / `SidecarDtoCodec.cs` (`garrisonPosts`, additive in v3), `Plugin.cs` (bind `Enable Garrison Manning`), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs` (`Garrison`, `GarrisonPost`); test `GarrisonPlannerTests.cs`.

**Vanilla to read:** 11668-11869 (capital need formula 11712-11741 reused for post threat), 17392-17485, 81931-81993, 82081-82093, 96676-96686 (`BattleUnits.fort`), 10458-10515, 14080-14158 (availability; the Postfix withholds Garrison), `IsWithinOperationsTheater` 17090-17110 (the post's theater box is set on arrival, as G5a's writer does), W&L 8271/8366/14381-14456.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public enum GarrisonPostKind { ObjectiveTown = 0, RailHub = 1, Port = 2, Depot = 3, Fort = 4 }
public struct GarrisonPost { public int PostId; public GarrisonPostKind Kind; public float X, Z; public float Need; public float ExistingStrength; public int AssignedInstanceId; /* in-session */ public string AssignedUnitKey; /* persisted */ public int QuietDays; }
public static class GarrisonPlanner {
  public const float ThreatShare = 0.5f;      // posted strength ≥ ThreatShare × post threat − existing garrison
  public const int MaxPostsPerAlliance = 6; public const int ReleaseQuietDays = 14;
  // Posts = own objective towns, rail hubs and ports (ObjectiveRole), own depots and own forts that lie in a Hold, Screen or Support sector
  // or within relocation range of a belief; the capital is excluded (the capital floor owns it). Threat = the 11712-11741 formula on beliefs.
  public static void PlanPosts(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, TheaterPlan plan, System.Collections.Generic.List<GarrisonPost> into);
  public static int PickFormation(CampaignSnapshot s, in GarrisonPost post, Allocation a); // smallest fighting formation that covers the need,
  // from Hold / EconomyOfForce / Reserve only; never MainEffort if that drops it below RequiredRatio, never CapitalGuard, Recon or Recover; -1 none
}
public sealed class GarrisonLedger { public void Sync(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<GarrisonPost> posts, Allocation a, int daySerial);
  public bool IsGarrison(int instanceId); public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<GarrisonPost> Posts { get; }
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<GarrisonPost> posts); }
```
Existing strength = the fort's garrison dummy (`groupstrengthdirect`) plus own formations within bugle range of the post. A post is released after `ReleaseQuietDays` with no belief within relocation range. The executor uses G5a's mover (hold point = the post, within bugle range) and sets the formation's theater box on arrival; vanilla's reactive recapture (job 29) is unchanged and complements it. W&L (F's chain classes): never a `PlayerCommand`; a `PlayerMapGroup` is order-only (`TryIssue`, intent `Redeploy`, type 5) and never the sole garrison of a post; a `CarryingGroup` may be posted (F's dispatch).

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first (`GarrisonPlannerTests`): `PostsOnlyOwnTownsHubsPortsDepotsForts`, `CapitalNeverAGarrisonPost`, `PostOnlyInThreatenedOrHoldSectors`, `NeedSubtractsFortGarrisonDummy`, `NeedFromBeliefsOnly`, `NeverFromMainEffortBelowRequiredRatio`, `NeverTakesCapitalGuardReconOrRecover`, `PrefersSmallestFormationCoveringNeed`, `PostsCappedPerAlliance`, `ReleasedAfterQuietDays`, `NeverPlayerCommandOrSoleMapGroupPlayer`, `GarrisonWithheldFromVanillaOperations`, `GarrisonPostsRoundTripInSidecar` (by `AssignedUnitKey`), `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`.
- [ ] Implement; scope `campaign.planner.garrison` (2.0). Telemetry `[Garrison] alliance=<a> posts=<n> manned=<n> released=<n> uncovered=<n>` on change; `[GarrisonPost] alliance=<a> post=<name> kind=<kind> unit=<name> state=<Moving|Holding|Released> need=<x>` per transition.
- [ ] Gates; docs (`patch-catalog` runtime row, `handoff`, spec component 9 as built); commit `feat(campaign-planner): garrison manning beyond the capital [G8]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness (red first); mutation plants: drop the capital exclusion → `CapitalNeverAGarrisonPost` red; omniscient threat → `NeedFromBeliefsOnly` red. In-game: a 3-month campaign session shows `[Garrison] … manned ≥ 1` in a threatened theater, a posted formation holding at a town or fort (`[GarrisonPost] … state=Holding`), and release after a quiet fortnight; vanilla's capital defense and recapture lines unchanged; no `[GarrisonPost]` naming the W&L player's command.

## G9 — Ordnance: re-arming priority and weapon purchasing (after G8; waits for `fix/arms-bugs`)

User direction 2026-10-15: "Fix all". Source: `research/armament.md` § B, C and recommendation 4; every anchor below re-read 2026-10-15 in the decompile (build 14703111).

**Vanilla, as read.**
- **Re-arming** (`CheckWeaponUpgrades` 16080-16153, from the alliance tick every `updaterhythmalliancedata` = 0.5 game-day, 81813, 81492): skips the player alliance outside `ai_vs_ai` (16082); copies `GameVars.alliance[a].unitlist` (16091; built in scene-hierarchy order, 81296-81306); on the new-campaign pass (`isinitialization`) drops garrisons and **shuffles** the list (16092-16131); drops `dlcw_isundercommander` units in a career (16137-16144); then `UpgradeWeaponForUnit` (16155-16193) per unit in list order: a basic unit takes `GetBestWeapon` (16224-16257), the highest-WSF model of its type with stock for the whole unit (strength + wounded + sick, or `statsguns`) that beats its current WSF, returning its old pieces to stock. So whoever comes first in the list takes the scarce new models. Nothing else in the call reads `unitlist` except a debug string (`unitlist.IndexOf`, 16181, 16187).
- **Purchasing** (`CheckPurchaseWeapons` 16259-16345, alliance tick 81757): skips the player alliance unless `automanageweapons` (in a W&L career: unless the player is CIC, 16263) and on a bad credit rating (`IsRatingOkForWeaponPurchases`); reads every enemy alliance's **true** `averageweaponstrengthguns/rifles` (16266-16282); per lane counts "equippable units" over models better than its own average (16287-16312) and orders when that count is below `aiminnumberofequippableunits` × artillery/other units (16325, 16335); the pick is **random** among researched-or-imported models with no open order that beat the own average (`list2`/`list`), or that beat the enemy average when behind (`list4`/`list3`) (16328, 16338); the amount is fixed (`usedstatsgunsdummy × aiweaponordersmultipleguns`, `unittypesize[0] × aiweaponordersmultiplerifles`). `WeaponList.PlaceWeaponOrder(alliance, weaponid, quantity)` (227136-227166) refuses a second open order or a missing capital, prices it with `GetProductionFigures` (64074-64142: domestic availability from stock, standardization, `productionmultiplier`, project 102; imports × blockade, 64120-64124; relations), takes the delivery time from `GetDeliveryTime(GameVars.playeralliance, …)` (63989-64001; F7 of the research: it reads the player alliance) and charges the full cost at once. Cost and delivery time never enter the choice.
- **Battle value.** Vanilla ranks by WSF (`GetWeaponStrengthFactor` 16214-16222: `range_yd × rpm^0.2 × firepower[0]/2 × accuracy³`), so rate of fire barely counts; in tactical battles reload time is `1 / rpm` (119633), so a repeater fires several times as often as a rifle-musket; autocalc ignores weapon rate of fire (21025-21047).
- **Researched but unproduced models (research F3): a vanilla bug.** Evidence: the player's order screen allows a model only when `AbleToOrder` (63938-63946) — `GetAvailability` (63962-63987) returns "Not produced" or tooltip 1831 for a researched model with `productionmultiplier <= 0` that is not imported; the AI's candidate test is researched-or-imported only (16296, 16313); 16289 holds a discarded `_ = j == 37 || j == 38;`, the remnant of an exclusion of exactly the Gatling (37) and Coffee Mill (38) (record order of `weapons.txt`: names at lines 1964 and 2017, 53 lines per record from record 40 at 2122); those models (and the Williams gun, id 12, line 639) exist as one-shot project stock (projects 10, 11). So the AI orders models the game says are not produced, at the floor availability (0.01, 64000) and the slowest delivery. The bug fix belongs to `fix/arms-bugs`; G9 never picks such a model either way (rule below).

**Decisions.**
1. **Re-arming priority (`RearmPriorityPatch`, Prefix + Finalizer on `AICampaign.CheckWeaponUpgrades(int alliance, bool isinitialization)`, snapshot-restore as #25):** for alliances 0/1 with a published allocation no older than 2 review days, outside the new-campaign pass (`isinitialization` → vanilla untouched) and while `ExecutorsPaused` is false, the Prefix saves `GameVars.alliance[a].unitlist` (the list object and its order) in `__state` and stably reorders it in place by the role of each unit's campaign group (`BattleUnits.GetCampaignGroup`, 82991; the allocation's `Assignment.Role`, a group-to-rank map built once per call from each assigned group's `allattachedunits`): MainEffort, Support, Reserve, Hold, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon, (G8) Garrison, unassigned, then Recover and in-transit (`MoveTracker.InTransit` or `FormationFlags.InTransfer`) last; vanilla order is kept inside a rank. The Finalizer writes the saved order back even when vanilla throws. Vanilla's per-unit rule, stock checks, cascade and its W&L skip run unchanged; no unit is re-armed by Whiskey. No skip.
2. **Purchase pick (`OrdnancePurchasePatch`, two classes):** a Prefix + Finalizer on `CheckPurchaseWeapons(int _alliance)` sets/clears a `[ThreadStatic]` scope (alliance 0/1, fresh plan, flag on, executors not paused, not the CIC player's alliance); a Prefix on `WeaponList.PlaceWeaponOrder(int alliance, ref int weaponid, int quantity)` edits the argument while the scope is set for that alliance: `weaponid` becomes `OrdnancePicker.Pick(...)` over **vanilla's own candidate set for that lane** (same `isari` as vanilla's pick; researched or imported; no open order; WSF above the own lane average) **∩ orderable** (`(IsResearched(a) && productionmultiplier > 0) || IsImported(a)`, the player screen's rule computed for the right alliance). Trigger, amount (`quantity` is not touched), rating gate, one-order-per-model, capital check and cost charging stay vanilla's. When the intersection is empty G9 leaves vanilla's pick (and logs it once): without a skip G9 cannot cancel an order, and the unproduced-model fix is `fix/arms-bugs`'s. **No skipping Prefix: nothing needs user approval.**
3. **Score (`OrdnancePicker`, pure, per lane, every term normalized to [0, 1] over the candidates):** `ValuePerCostWeight 0.35 × battleValue / unitCost` + `DeliveryWeight 0.20 × (1 − deliveryYears / 2)` + `BattleValueWeight 0.20 × battleValue` + `StandardizationWeight 0.15 × standardization` + `EnemyEdgeWeight 0.10 × [battleValue beats the believed enemy lane quality]`, where `battleValue = range_yd × rpm_pract × firepower[0]/2 × accuracy³` (vanilla's WSF terms with rate of fire at full power, as tactical reload does, 119633; guns keep vanilla's tube-weight divisor), `unitCost = GetProductionFigures(a, id, quantity).cost / quantity` (read-only: `Economy.ConsumeWeapons(…, consumegoods: false)` is a dry run, 31486; the import share already carries the blockade factor, 64120-64124, so the cost and the delivery time are blockade-aware), `deliveryYears = GetDeliveryTime(GameVars.playeralliance, quantity, id, figures)` (the value vanilla's `PlaceWeaponOrder` itself uses), `standardization = Weapon.standardizationforalliance[a]` (64154-64190: equipped + stock of that model). **Fog:** the believed enemy lane quality is the confidence-weighted mean of `EnemyBelief.RifleQuality/GunQuality` (new fields recorded at sighting time by G1's detection runtime: the strength-weighted WSF of the seen group's armed units, `GetWeaponStrengthFactor(group)` 16195-16212 split by lane; never refreshed while unseen); G9 never reads another alliance's `averageweaponstrength*`. Ties by lower model id (deterministic).
4. **W&L.** The re-arm reorder never changes a unit's weapons, and vanilla already drops the player's units from the loop (16137-16144); in a CIC career the planner stands down for that alliance, so both patches leave it to vanilla. The purchase steer acts only for alliances the planner runs (it is alliance-level buying, never the player's own command's arms); a CIC player's alliance (vanilla's own 16263 gate, plus the stand-down) is never steered.
5. **Dependencies:** G4 (`Allocation`, roles), G5a (`MoveTracker.InTransit`), G8 (Garrison role rank), G1 (beliefs; G9 adds the two quality fields to `EnemyDetection`/`EnemyBelief` and fills them in `EnemyDetectionRuntime`), and `fix/arms-bugs` merged (cross-stage gates). It does not fit earlier: it needs G8's role and G1's belief fields, and both patches read the published allocation.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/RearmPriority.cs` (pure: `RankOf(FormationRole?, bool recover, bool inTransit)`, `StableOrder(int[] ranks) → int[]`), `Strategic/Planner/OrdnancePicker.cs` (pure: `Candidate { Id, IsArtillery, Orderable, OpenOrder, Wsf, BattleValue, UnitCost, DeliveryYears, Standardization }`, `Pick(lane candidates, vanillaPick, believedEnemyQuality) → id`, `BattleValue(range, rpm, firepower0, accuracy, isArtillery, tubeWeight)`), `Strategic/Planner/OrdnanceRuntime.cs` (runtime: candidate facts from `GameVars.weapon`, believed enemy quality), `Patches/RearmPriorityPatch.cs`, `Patches/OrdnancePurchasePatch.cs` (two classes); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`EnemyDetection`/`EnemyBelief` quality fields), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyDetectionRuntime.cs` (record them), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyArmyModel.cs` (carry them), `Plugin.cs` (bind `Enable Rearm Priority`, `Enable Ordnance Purchase Steering`, default on), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs` (`Ordnance` Decision, `OrdnancePurchase` Write), `tools/smoke-situational.txt` (the purchase ids); test `OrdnanceTests.cs`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first (`OrdnanceTests`, red first): `RearmOrderFollowsAllocationRole`, `RearmOrderStableWithinRole`, `RecoverAndTransitRearmLast`, `UnassignedUnitsAfterAssignedRoles`, `RearmRestoresUnitListOrder` (pure snapshot/restore over a list), `RearmLeavesInitializationPassToVanilla`, `RearmStandsDownWithStaleOrMissingPlan`, `RearmStandsDownWhileJobMachinePaused`, `PurchaseKeepsVanillaLaneAndQuantity`, `PurchasePicksOnlyFromVanillaCandidatesThatAreOrderable`, `ResearchedUnproducedModelNeverPicked` (Gatling 37 / Coffee Mill 38 / Williams with `productionmultiplier` 0), `PurchaseLeavesVanillaPickWhenNoOrderableCandidate`, `PurchasePrefersValuePerCost`, `PurchaseRateOfFireCountsInFull` (a repeater with lower WSF beats a rifle-musket on `battleValue`), `PurchasePenalizesSlowDelivery`, `PurchasePrefersStandardizedModel`, `PurchaseBlockadeShowsInImportCost` (higher blockade → higher unit cost/delivery for an import-only model → lower score), `PurchaseEnemyEdgeFromBeliefsOnly` (source pin: `OrdnanceRuntime.cs`/`OrdnancePurchasePatch.cs` contain no `averageweaponstrength` read; an unseen enemy gives no edge term), `PurchaseNeverForCicPlayersAlliance`, `PurchaseDeterministicOnTies`, `DetectionRecordsSightedWeaponQuality`, `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`.
- [ ] Implement pure files, runtime, patches (scopes `campaign.patch.ordnance-rearm.prefix|finalizer`, `campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.scope.prefix|finalizer`, `campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.pick`; `PatchFirstFire.Mark` literals; the two purchase ids in `tools/smoke-situational.txt`: only when vanilla's trigger fires). Telemetry `[Ordnance] alliance=<a> rearm main=<n> support=<n> reserve=<n> hold=<n> capital=<n> last=<n>` on change; `[OrdnancePurchase] alliance=<a> lane=<guns|rifles> vanilla=<id> picked=<id> score=<s> cost=<c> delivery=<y> reason=<value|delivery|std|edge|vanilla-kept>` per order.
- [ ] Docs: `docs/patch-catalog.md` rows (ordinals at G7 merge order), `docs/bug-fixes/README.md` F3 status (owned by `fix/arms-bugs`), `docs/handoff.md`.
- [ ] Gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): ordnance re-arming priority and scored weapon purchase [G9]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness (red first; mutation plants: rank table reversed → `RearmOrderFollowsAllocationRole` red; Finalizer restore skipped → `RearmRestoresUnitListOrder` red; orderable filter dropped → `ResearchedUnproducedModelNeverPicked` red; rate of fire at vanilla's 0.2 power → `PurchaseRateOfFireCountsInFull` red; enemy edge from `averageweaponstrength` → `PurchaseEnemyEdgeFromBeliefsOnly` red). In game: smoke below.

**Smoke (G9):**
1. `LOG`: `[first-fire] campaign.patch.ordnance-rearm.prefix` and `.finalizer` within the first game-day; `[first-fire] campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.pick` once vanilla places an AI order (situational).
2. `SESSION`: `[Ordnance] alliance=1 rearm main=<n> …` rows; vanilla's debug log (`GameVars.DebugOwnLog`, when enabled) shows `upgrade weapon for unit …` lines for main-effort units before quiet-front units in the same tick.
3. `[OrdnancePurchase] alliance=1 lane=rifles vanilla=<id> picked=<id> …` with `picked` never a model whose order screen shows "Not produced"; vanilla's `AI:Placing rifle order … weaponid:<vanilla>` followed by `taking weapon order 1 id:<picked> qty:<vanilla's quantity>`.
4. W&L corps career: no `[Ordnance]`/`[OrdnancePurchase]` effect on the player's own command's units (their weapons change only by the player); W&L CIC career: no G9 rows for the player's alliance.
5. Rollback: `Enable Rearm Priority = false` → unit list order unchanged (no `[Ordnance]` rows); `Enable Ordnance Purchase Steering = false` → `picked` absent, vanilla's random pick.
